ippulavar peyarkaḷuḷ cila vaṭamoḻip peyarkaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cātēvaṉār, iṉicanta nākaṉār, ukkirap peruvaḻuti, urōṭōkam, ulōccaṉ, kantarattaṉ, kōcaṉ, kapilaṉ, kācipaṉ, cēkam (pūtaṉār); ciṟumōlikaṉār, āttirēyaṉ, tēvaṉ, mētāviyār, nikaṇṭaṉ, tattaṉ, parāyaṉ, piramacāri, kucikaṉ, pōtaṉ, ācāṉ ivaṟṟi nōkka, innūṟpākkaḷ pāṭappeṟṟa kālattil vaṭamoḻi yāḷar enta aḷavu tamiḻakattil celvākkup peṟṟiruntaṉar eṉpatu ōraḷavu aṟiyappaṭum. kācipaṉ, āttirēyaṉ, parāyaṉ, nikaṇṭaṉ, piramacāri, kucikaṉ, ulōccaṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷōṭu viḷaṅkiyavar vaṭamoḻiyāḷareṉṟē koḷḷalām. āyiṉum avarkaḷ tamiḻarōṭu kalantu, tamiḻarāy vāḻntu, tamiḻp pulavarāy viḷaṅki, naṟṟamiḻil naṉku pāṭiyuḷḷaṉar eṉpatu maṟakkaṟ pālataṉṟu.
aracaṉāl kāvitip paṭṭam peṟṟa vaṇikap perumakkaḷum, aṟuvi vaṇikar pōṉṟa palatuṟi vaṇikap perumakkaḷum, ciṟupiḷḷikaḷukkup pāṭam kaṟpippavar mutal palarukkum kalvi kaṟpikkum āciriyaṉmārkaḷum, kuṟatti mutaliya tiṇinilamakkaḷum, kollaṉ mutaliya palavakit toḻilāḷarum, nallicip pulavarāyt tikaḻntaṉar eṉpatu ip pulavar paṭṭiyalik koṇṭu iṉituṇarappaṭum. iṅṅaṉam makkaḷ iṉattil evvita vēṟupāṭum iṉṟi ellāt tarattiṉarum pulavarkaḷāy viḷaṅkiya caṅka kālam tamiḻar varalāṟṟil poṟkālam eṉṟu kūṟuvatil taṭiyuṇṭō?
iṉi innūluḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa ceytikaḷik kāṇpōm:
nūṟceytikaḷ
aracarum ciṟṟaracarum: kuṟuntokip pākkaḷil kuṟikkappaṭṭāṟ pōlavē cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiyar tamakkuriya potup peyarkaḷāl naṟṟiṇip pākkaḷil kuṟikkappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉar. ālaṅkāṉattuc ceḻiyaṉ (387) eṉṟu neṭuñ ceḻiyaṉ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷāṉ. ciṟṟaracaruḷ ōri (6, 52, 265, 320), paḻiyaṉ (10), tittaṉ (58), aḻici (87), maliyamāṉ (100, 170, 291), periyaṉ (131), āy aṇṭiraṉ (167, 238), aṉṉi (180), pāri (253), virāaṉ (260, 350), naṉṉaṉ (270, (391), miñili (265), taḻumpaṉ (300), vāṇaṉ (340), arumaṉ (367) neṭumāṉañci (381), muṭiyaṉ (390) eṉpavar kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉar.
araciyal: aracar tam kaṭamiyi aṟintu naṭappārāyiṉ, avaratu āṭci iṉpantarum kuḷirnta niḻalip pōṉṟatu (146). cōḻar aṟam keṭātu nīti vaḻaṅkupavar (400). pōrkkaḷattil kaḷam pāṭuvārum, icik karuviyi icippavarkaḷum kūṭi iruntu veṟṟi peṟṟa aracaṉi vāḻttuvatu vaḻakkam (113).
kuṟiñcinila ūrkaḷil ūrkkāval iruntatu. kāvalar kuṟiñci eṉṉum paṇṇip pāṭikkoṇṭu iravu muḻumiyum tūṅkāmal ūrikkāval kāttaṉar (255). neytal nila ūrkaḷilum kāvalar yāmantōṟum maṇiyaṭittu ōci yeḻuppi, talikkaṭi, puḻikkaṭi vāyilkaḷip pātu kāttuk koḷḷuṅkaḷ, eṉṟu kūṟicceṉṟaṉar (132).
takuti uṭiyavar peyariyum avar vāḻum ūriyum ēṭṭil varintu ūrppotu maṉṟattil vittiruppatu vaḻakkam (365).
āyaṉ: āyaṉ mulli malari iravilē koytu paṉaṅkuruttiṉ pōḻuṭaṉē cērttu māliyākat toṭuttu aṇivāṉ (169). avaṉ palavākiya kāliṭṭup piṉṉiya melliya uṟiyuṭaṉē, tīkkaṭik kōl mutaliya karuvikaḷi iṭṭu vitta tōl piyic curukkik kaṭṭip paṉiyōlip pāyōṭu mutukiṟ kaṭṭiyiruppāṉ; pāl vili kūṟi viṟpāṉ; kiyil kōlūṉṟi iruppāṉ; akkōlmītu oru kāli vittu oṭuṅkiya niliyil niṟpāṉ; vāyik kuvittu vīḷi eṉappaṭum aḻittalākiya kuṟiyi eḻuppuvāṉ. atu kēṭṭa āṭukaḷ piṟanilam pukāmal niṟkum (142). āyar tayirt tāḻiyiṉ muṭināṟṟam nīṅka viḷampaḻam iṭṭu vippar (12).
paratavar vīṭukaḷ paṉiyōlikaḷālum, ilikaḷālum, taḻikaḷālum vēyappaṭum. paṉi ōlikaḷōṭu muṭkaḷic cērttuk kaṭṭappaṭṭa vēli ūric cūḻntu irukkum (38); paratavar timiṅkilattiyum vēṭṭiyāṭuvar; mīṉkaḷip piṭittu viṭiyaṟkāliyil koṇṭu varuvar; puṉṉimara niḻalil iruntu tēṉ maṇam vīcum teḷinta kaḷḷip parukuvar (328); cila camayaṅkaḷil kaṭalilēyē cuṟā mīṉkaḷiyum vēṟu periya mīṉkaḷiyum tam timililēyē tuṇṭittu iṟiccikaḷi nirappik koṇṭu karikku varuvar (112).
ūrkaḷ: innūṟ pāṭalkaḷil kīḻvarum ūrkaḷ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa: toṇṭi  cēraṉukkuriya tuṟi muka nakaram (8, 195), pōr - paḻiyaṉ eṉṟa ciṟṟaracaṉukku uriyatu (10), koṟki-pāṇṭiyar tuṟimuka nakaram (23), mānti-cēranāṭṭuk kaṭaṟkari ūr (35; 395), kāṇṭa vāyil  kaṭaṟkari ūr kaṭal-pāṇṭiyar talinakaram (39, 298); kiṭaṅkil (65), cāykkāṭu (83). poṟiyāṟu (131), ampar (141), ārkkāṭu (190), maruṅkūrp paṭṭiṉam-pāṇṭiya nāṭṭuk kaṭaṟkari nakaram (358), puṉalvāyil (260), iruppiyūr (260), pāram (265), āṟēṟu (265). kuṉṟūr (280), kaḻāar (281), muḷḷūr (291), ūṇūr (300), vāṇaṉ ciṟukuṭi (340), arumaṉ ciṟukuṭi (367), kuṭanti vāyil3 (379), veṇṇi (390).
uṭi: kaliṅkam, pūṅkaliṅkam, tukil, nuṇtukil, amtukil eṉpaṉa uyarnta melliya āṭi vakikaḷ (20, 43, 90, 120, 366), pālinilattu āṭavar tuvar āṭiyi uṭuttiyiruntaṉar (33). vīrar iṭiyil kaccu aṇintaṉar (21). kāṉavar (kuṟiñci nilattu āṭavar) marappaṭṭi nārāl piṉṉiya uṭiyi uṭuttiyiruntaṉar (64). avartam makaḷir taḷirkaḷiyum pūṅkottukkaḷiyum nerukkamāka vittuk kaṭṭappeṟṟa taḻikaḷi āṭiyāka uṭuttiyiruntaṉar (204). nāri iṟakiṉ meṉmiyum, veṇmiyum vāynta āṭikaḷum vaḻakkil iruntaṉa (70).
vāṇikam: pala nāṭukaḷiliruntum kappalkaḷ vantu tuṟimukattil taṅkuvatu vaḻakkam (293). kaṭitteru niyamam eṉappaṭṭatu (45). tamiḻ vaṇikar vāṇikattiṉ poruṭṭu vaṭanāṭṭiṟkuc ceṉṟaṉar; kaṅkiyāṟṟil kalattil ceṉṟaṉar (189). paratavar mīṉi viṟṟuk kaḷḷip peṟṟaṉar. uppu vāṇikar uppi viṟṟu nellip peṟṟaṉar (183). ivaṟṟi nōkka ak kālattil paṇṭamāṟṟu vaḻakkil iruntami teḷivākum.
kalikaḷ: tamiḻakattil ōviyar iruntaṉar (188, 146, 177, 182, 68). pāṇar cīṟiyāḻ (38), pēriyāḻ (40) vācittaṉar. muḻavu (67), mayirkkaṇ muracu (93), taṇṇumi (130), kiṇippāṟi (108), toṇṭakac ciṟupaṟi (104), kuṭamuḻā4 (220), kuḻal (69) mutaliya icik karuvikaḷ vaḻakkil iruntaṉa. kuṟiñcinila makaḷir nelmutaliyavaṟṟik kuṟṟumpōtu pāṭikkoṇṭē kuṟṟiṉar (79).
maruttuvaṉ: aṟavōṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ (136) nōyi ārāyntu annōykkut takka maruntiyē koṭuttāṉ (136). cōtiṭam pārttavar kaṇiyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. eṉavē, cōtiṭakkaliyum caṅkakālat tamiḻakattil iruntatu eṉpatu teḷivākum (373).
camayam : akkāla makkaḷ tittiṅkaḷ mutal nāḷil nīrāṭi nōṉpu muṟṟiyiruntu uṇṭaṉar (22). ti mātattil kuḷirnta nīril nīrāṭip peṇkaḷ nōṉpiruntaṉar (80). nīṇṭacaṭiyiyum aciyāta meyyiyum koṇṭu maliyil tavam ceypavar (tavaciyar) akkālattil iruntaṉar (141). kaṭaṟkariyil palavakik koṭikaḷ paṭarnta iṭaṅkaḷil akkoṭikaḷi aṟuttu avviṭaṅkaḷil nōṉpiṉik koṇṭa mātar uṟivatu vaḻakkam. avarkaḷ paṭiva makaḷir eṉappaṭṭaṉar (272). ivarkaḷ kavuntiyaṭikaḷ pōṉṟa camaṇa camayap peṇ tuṟavikaḷākalām.
paṇpāṭu
tēriṉ uruḷiyil naṇṭukaḷ akappaṭṭu nacuṅkāta paṭi pākaṉ tēric celuttiṉāṉ (11) eṉpatu avaṉukkirunta aruḷ uṇarcciyi uṇarttukiṟatu. caṅkakāla makkaḷ celvatti nillāp poruḷ eṉṟaṉar (126, 241); maṉṉāp poruḷ (71) eṉṟaṉar; iṉpamum, iḷamiyum virivil kaḻivaṉa (46) eṉṉum uṇmiyi aṟintiruntaṉar. periyōr koṭiya tīttoḻilic ceytavariṭattu acceyali nērilē kaṇṭum uḷḷattāl ārāyntum attoḻil ceytōr iṉi ataṉic ceyyātu oḻiyumpaṭi palavāṟu aṟivuri kūṟiṉar (146). ōṉṟaṉ kāraṇamāka uṇṭāṉati vēṟoṉṟaṉ mēl iṭṭuk kūṟutal paṇpuṭiyataṉṟu (117), viruntiṉarkaḷukku uṇavup poruḷkaḷi pakirntu koṭuttal ciṟanta paṇpāṭu (135) eṉpavaṟṟit tamiḻ makkaḷ aṟintiruntaṉar. iravil viruntu variṉum uvakiyōṭu upacarittaṉar (142).
tam vāḻnāḷum, poruḷum piṟar poruṭṭē eṉṟu eṇṇi aruḷmikka neñcuṭaṉē tamiḻar poruḷīṭṭac ceṉṟaṉar (186, 286). illiṉ kaṇ ceyalaṟṟu iruppavarkku immikkuriya pukaḻum, irumikku uriya iṉpamum, maṟumiyilum iṉpuṟuvataṟku ētuvākiya irantōrkku ītal mutaliya koṭimiyum, ākiya mūṉṟum kikūṭuvatilli (214). pukaḻ mikumpaṭi aruḷ uṇarcciyōṭu vāḻkiṉṟavaratu celvam polivaṭiyum (217). makkaḷ maram paṭṭup pōkumpaṭi ataṉiṭamuḷḷa marunti muṟṟum koḷḷār (226). viruntiṉari upacarittal uyarnta paṇpāṭu (258, 280, 281). āyuḷ mutirntu yākki mūttut taḷarntavar iḷamiyi virumpiṉ aṭital iyalātu; tam vāḻnāḷiṉ elliyi evarum aṟiyār (134). naṭpiṉkaṇ ciṟutavaṟum nērātapaṭi naṭattal vēṇṭum. (315). tīyaneṟi narakam pōṉṟatu (329). ivi tamiḻartam uyarnta karuttukkaḷ.
piriviṭi āṟṟāḷākiya talimakaḷ tōḻiyi nōkki, ivvulakattil piṟantavar iṟappar eṉpatu uṇmi. kaṇavaṉ pirivāl iṟappu vantatē eṉṟu nāṉ añcavilli. avvāṟu iṟappiṉ, iṉivarum eṉatu piṟappu makkaṭ piṟappu illāmal vēṟu oru piṟappāka māṟiviṭiṉ, eṉ kātalaṉi appoḻutu maṟappēṉō eṉṟu añcukiṉṟēṉ (397), eṉṟu uḷḷam uruka urittāḷ. uyir pala piṟavikaḷ eṭukkum eṉṟa nampikki akkālattil iruntatu eṉpatiyum, uṇmi maṉivi pala piṟavikaḷilum taṉ kaṇavaṉiyē peṟavirumpu vatiyum it taliviyiṉ kūṟṟu uṇarttukiṉṟatu allavā?
mēṟkōḷ
naṟṟiṇip pāṭalkaḷil tirukkuṟaḷ karuttukkaḷum coṟkaḷum coṟṟoṭarkaḷum āṅkāṅku amintuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟi niralē kīḻē kāṇka:

uvami mutaliyaṉa
naṟṟiṇip pāṭalkaḷil uḷḷattik kavarum uvamikaḷ āṅkāṅku iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa (84, 97, 131, 184, 345). taṟkuṟippēṟṟam (242), piṟitu moḻital (266) pōṉṟa piṟa aṇikaḷ cilavum iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivi paṭittuc cuvittarutaṟkuriyaṉa.
vaṭacoṟkaḷ
naṟṟiṇip pāṭalkaḷil mikaccila vaṭacoṟkaḷē iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ vatuvi (125), tavaciyar (141), cāpam (288), piracam-vaṇṭu (268), tāmam (232), aṟciram (312) eṉpavi kuṟippiṭat takkavi.
13. akanāṉūṟu
muṉṉuri
akanāṉūṟṟup pāṭalkaḷiṉ ciṟumi patiṉmūṉṟu aṭi; perumi muppattoṉṟu. itaṟku neṭuntoki eṉṟum peyar uṇṭu. itaṉit tokuppittavaṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ ukkirap peruvaḻuti. tokuttavaṉ maturi urippūrikuṭi kiḻāṉ makaṉ uruttiracaṉmaṉ. innūlil mutal nūṟṟirupatu pākkaḷ kaḷiṟṟiyāṉi niri eṉavum, 121 mutal 300 variyil uḷḷavi maṇimiṭi pavaḻam eṉavum, iṟuti nūṟu nittilak kōvi eṉṟum peyar peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ic ceytikaḷ innūṟpāyirattil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
ip pāyirattip pāṭiyavaṉ iṭiyaḷa nāṭṭu maṇakkuṭiyāṉ pālvaṇṇa tēvaṉāṉa villava tariyaṉ. ivaṉ kālam aṟiyumāṟilli. ivaṉ, innūlil pāṭiyuḷḷa pulavar nūṟṟu nāṟpattivar eṉak kūṟiyuḷḷāṉ. iṅṅaṉam ivaṉ kūṟiya pulavar eṇṇikkiyiyē ilakkiya varalāṟu eḻutiyōr aṉivarum tattam nūlil kuṟittuḷḷaṉar. pulavar peyarkaḷi eḻutik kaṇakkup pārkkumpōtu, avarkaḷ toki nūṟṟimpatteṭṭu1 ākiṟatu. mūvar peyarkaḷ teriyavilli.
aintiṇi paṟṟiya ippāṭalkaḷiṉ vippu muṟi kavaṉittataṟkuriyatu. ōṟṟi eṇpaṭa varupavi pāli paṟṟiya pākkaḷ; 10, 20, 30 eṉṉum eṇkaḷip peṟṟu varupavi neytal paṟṟiyavi; 4, 14, 24 eṉa iṟutiyil 4 eṉṉum eṇṇip peṟṟu varupavi mulli paṟṟiyavi; 2, 8, 12, 18 eṉa 2, 8 eṉṉum eṇkaḷi iṟutiyil peṟṟu varupavi kuṟiñcit tiṇiyip paṟṟiyavi; 6, 16, 26, 36 mutaliyavi marutam paṟṟiya pākkaḷ. ittakiya tokuppumuṟi kuṟuntokiyil illi; naṟṟiṇiyilum illi. eṉavē avi iraṇṭiṟkum piṉṉarē innūl tokukkap peṟṟirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu niṉittal takum. kuṟuntokikku maṟutaliyāṉa neṭuntoki eṉṉum peyarum, ippāṭalkaḷ piṉpu tokukkap peṟṟavi eṉpati valiyuṟuttuvatākum.
akanānūṟṟup pāṭalkaḷuḷ kuṟiñci paṟṟiyavi 80; pāli paṟṟiyavi 200; mulli paṟṟiyavi 40; marutam paṟṟiyavi; 40; neytal paṟṟiyavi 40.
innūlukkup paḻiya uri oṉṟu uṇṭu. atu, mutal toṇṇūṟu pāṭalkaḷukku maṭṭumē kuṟippuriyāka uḷḷatu. aṭuttu 70 ceyyuṭkaḷukku innūliṉ (akanānūṟṟiṉ) mutaṟ patippāciriyarāṉa vē. irāca kōpāla aiyaṅkār eṉṟa aṟiñar uri eḻutiyuḷḷār. piṉṉar, nūl muḻumikkum nāvalar na. mu. vēṅkaṭacāmi nāṭṭār avarkaḷum, karantik kaviyaracu rā. vēṅkaṭācalam piḷḷiyavarkaḷum cērntu uri eḻutiyuḷḷaṉar ivvuriyiṉāl innūliṉ ciṟappu naṉku viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu.
ip pāṭalkaḷil kuṟiñci, pāli, marutam, neytal eṉṉum aivaki nilaṅkaḷiṉ iyalpukaḷ viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; puṇartal, pirital, iruttal, ūṭal, iraṅkal eṉṉum aivaki oḻukkaṅkaḷ takka uvamikaḷuṭaṉ iṇiyaṟṟa muṟiyil teḷivuṟuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pāṭalkaḷ coṟceṟivum poruṭceṟivum uṭiyavi.

135.	maturip pullaṅ kaṇṇaṉār	
136.	maturi marutaṅkiḻār makaṉār peruṅ kaṇṇaṉār	
137.	maturi maḷḷaṉār	
138.	muḷḷiyūrp pūtiyār	
139.	mōcikkariyaṉār	
140.	maturi aḷakkar ñāḻār makaṉār ammaḷḷaṉār	
141.	maturi iḷaṅ kucikaṉār	
142.	maturik kaṇṇattaṉār	
143.	maturik kūttaṉār	
144.	maturit tattaṅkaṇṇaṉār	
145.	maturip poṉcey kollaṉ veṇṇākaṉār	
146.	maturi marutaṅkiḻār makaṉār peruṅkaṇṇaṉār	
147.	maruṅkūrp pākic cāttaṉ pūtaṉār	
148.	mōcikīraṉār

151.	vaṭamōtaṅkiḻār

varalāṟṟuc ceytikaḷ
nantarkaḷ (ki. mu. 425-ki. mu. 323)
akanāṉūṟu 265 ām pāṭalil nantarkaḷip paṟṟiya kuṟippuk kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
palpukaḻ niṟinta velpōr nantar
cīrmiku pāṭalik kuḻiik kaṅki
nīrmutal karanta nitiyam kollō.
251 ām pāṭalil iraṇṭu varikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
nantaṉ veṟukki yeytiṉum maṟṟavaṇ
taṅkalar vāḻi tōḻi.
ivviraṇṭu kuṟippukkaḷum māmūlaṉār eṉṟa pulavar perumāṉāl tarappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. nantar makatanāṭṭi āṇṭavar, avarkaḷ talinakaram pāṭaliputtiram. avarkaḷ celvattil ciṟantavarkaḷ. avarkaḷ ac celvattik kaṅkiyāṟṟiṉ aṭiyil putittu vittiruntaṉar. ic ceytiyi ivviraṇṭu pākkaḷiṉ aṭikaḷum kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa.
mōriyar paṭiyeṭuppu (ki. mu. 301-ki. mu. 273)
akanāṉūṟṟuc ceyyuṭkaḷ mūṉṟil (69, 281, 375) mōriyar (avarkkuttuṇiyāka vanta vaṭukar) paṭiyeṭupput tamiḻakattil nikaḻntami kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. puṟanāṉūṟṟilum itu paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.2 ip paṭiyeṭuppuc cantirakupta mōriyaṉ makaṉāṉa pintucāraṉ kālattil nikaḻntirukkalām eṉṟu varalāṟṟāciriyar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.3
nantarkaḷip paṟṟiya ceytiyum mōriyar paṭi yeṭuppip paṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷum intiya varalāṟṟuc ciṟappuṭiya ceytikaḷākum. caṅka kālap pulavarkaḷ ic ceytikaḷi enta aḷavu aṟintiruntaṉar eṉpati akanāṉūṟṟup pāṭalkaḷ aḻakuṟa aṟivikkiṉṟaṉa.
pēraracar: akanānūṟṟup pāṭalkaḷ akapporuḷ paṟṟiyaviyāyiṉum, avaṟṟuḷ tamiḻp pēraracar, ciṟṟaracar paṟṟiya ceytikaḷum piṟavum uvamikaḷākavum piṟavākavum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ic ceytikaḷ tamiḻaka varalāṟṟiṟkup peruntuṇi ceyvaṉavākum. iṅṅaṉam varalāṟṟuc ceytikaḷi eṭuttuk kūṟum akap poruḷ nūlkaḷuḷ akanāṉūṟu tali ciṟantatu.
cēra vēntaruḷ utiyaṉ cēral (65), cēralātaṉ (127), māntaram poṟiyaṉ kaṭuṅkō (142), utiyaṉ (168), kōti mārpaṉ (346) eṉpavar peyarkaḷāl cuṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉar. piṟar, kuṭṭuvaṉ (91, 296), vāṉavaṉ (308), vāṉavarampaṉ (383) eṉac cērarkkuriya potup peyarkaḷāl kuṟikkappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉar. coḻaruḷ tīttaṉ (6), karikālaṉ (141), kiḷḷi vaḷavaṉ (346) pēcappaṭṭuḷḷaṉar. palaviṭaṅkaḷil cōḻar eṉṟē potuppaṭiyākak kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉar. pāṇṭiyaruḷ ālaṅkāṉattuc ceḻiyaṉ (36), pacumpūṇ pāṇṭiyaṉ (162), paḻiyaṉ māṟaṉ (346) eṉpavar iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉar piṟa pāṇṭiyar vaḻuti (312), ceḻiyaṉ (335), kavuriyar (342), teṉṉavaṉ (342) eṉap pāṇṭiyarkkuriya potup peyarkaḷāl kuṟikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉar. toṇṭi nāṭṭi āṇṭa tiriyaṉ (85) potup peyarāl kuṟikkappeṟṟuḷḷāṉ.
ciṟṟaracar : vēḷ āvi (1), matti (6), kōṭip porunaṉ (13), kāri (35), titiyaṉ, eḻuni, erumiyūraṉ, iruṅkō vēṇmāṉ, porunaṉ (36), naṉṉaṉ, ēṟṟi, atti, kaṅkaṉ, kaṭṭi, kuṉṟuṟi, paḻiyaṉ, kaṇiyaṉ (44, 186, 326); aṉṉi (45), paṇṇaṉ (54, 177), pulli (61), vēḷ āy (69), akuti (76), piṭṭaṉ (77, 143), pāri (78), koṅkar (79), kaṭalaṉ (81), naṉṉaṉ vēṇmāṉ (97), periyaṉ (100), pāṇaṉ (Banan) (113, 226, 325), vēḷ evvi (115), añci (115), kaḻuvuḷ (135), āy eyiṉaṉ (148), tittaṉ veḷiyaṉ, piṇṭaṉ, naḷḷi, āy (152), miñili (181) ātaṉ eḻiṉi (216), vaḻutuṇittaḻumpaṉ (277), mucuṇṭi (249) (vaṭukar perumakaṉ) erumi (253), pēkaṉ (263), (nīṭūr kiḻavaṉ) evvi (266), aviyaṉ (271), pāri (303), atiyaṉ (325).
pēraracar pēraracaruṭaṉ pōriṭṭapōtu, avaravaric cērnta ciṟṟaracarum tattam pēraracar cārpil niṉṟu pōriṭṭaṉar (36); ciṟṟaracar oruvariyoruvar etirttup pōriṭṭaṉar; palar cērntu cilari etirttaṉar; cilar cērntu palari etirttaṉar; palar cērntu oruvaṉi etirttaṉar. ittakiya vivaraṅkaḷ innūṟ pāṭaṟkaḷil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
pāṇarkaḷ : pāṇarkaḷ (Banas) nāṉku pāṭalkaḷil (113, 226, 325, 386) kuṟikkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉar. tamiḻakattiṟku vaṭakkil avartam nāṭu iruntatu. avarkaḷ āṇṭa nāṭu, kiḻakkil karnūl māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa tirupparuppatam malikaḷ variyiluḷḷa nilappakutiyiyum, teṉ kiḻakkil cittūr (ciṟṟūr) māvaṭṭattiṉ mēṟkup pakuti variyiluḷḷa nilap pakutiyiyum, mēṟkē kōlār māvaṭṭattiyum taṉṉakattē koṇṭatu. toṭakkattil avarkaḷ āṭciyilirunta nilappakuti aṭarnta kāṭukaḷākavum pāṟikaḷ niṟinta pakutiyākavum iruntatu.4 piṉpu kālap pōkkil avarkaḷ nāṭu vaṭa ārkkāṭu māvaṭṭattiṉ vaṭamēṟkup pakutiyum cittūr māvaṭṭattiṉ mēṟkup pakutiyum cērnta nilapparappāyiṟṟu. appakuti perumpāṇap pāṭi eṉṟu piṟkālak kalveṭṭukkaḷil peyar peṟṟatu.5
maṇimēkali eṉṉum tamiḻk kāviyam-neṭumuṭik kiḷḷi eṉṟa cōḻavēntaṉ pāṇa aracar makaḷāṉa cīrtti eṉpavaḷi maṇantukoṇṭāṉ eṉṟum, avaḷ utaya kumāraṉukkut tāyeṉṟum kūṟukiṟatu. itaṉāl ki. pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakutiyilēyē pāṇa aracarkaḷ cōḻarkkup peṇ tarum takuti peṟṟiruntami teḷivākum.
kaṅkar: kaṅkar caṅka kālattilēyē vāḻnta araca marapiṉar (44, 186). avar marapiṉarē piṟkālattil taḻikkāṭṭi talinakarāyk koṇṭu āṇṭaṉar eṉṟu kūṟalām.
tiriyar: tiriyar toṇṭi maṇṭalatti āṇṭavar. avar talinakaram pavattiri (340). toṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ teṉpakutiyik kāñciyiliruntu iḷantiriyaṉ āṇṭu vantāṉ.
ūrāṭci : caṅka kālattil tērtal muṟiyāl ūrāṭci maṉṟa uṟuppiṉarkaḷ ūr makkaḷāl tērnteṭukkappaṭṭaṉar (77). ūrāṭci maṉṟattār ūr vaḻakkukaḷi vicārittu nīti vaḻaṅkiṉar (256).
ciṟappuc ceytikaḷ : tamiḻaracar āṭciyil oṟṟar iruntaṉar (313); tūtar iruntaṉar (337); aracāṅka varuvāy kuṟippiṭṭa ūril vittup pātukākkappaṭṭatu. cōḻa nāṭṭu varuvāy kuṭantiyil vittup pātukākkappaṭṭatu (60). cōḻaratu talinakarāṉa uṟiyūrilirunta aṟaṅkūṟu aviyam, muṟi vaḻaṅkuvatil peyar peṟṟatu (63).
tamiḻar tirumaṇam: akanāṉūṟṟup pāṭalkaḷ iraṇṭu caṅkakālat tamiḻariṉ tirumaṇattip paṟṟip pēcukiṉṟaṉa. (1) variciyāyk kālkaḷiyuṭiya kuḷirnta periya pantaril maṇal parappappaṭṭiruntatu. pantalic cuṟṟilum malar mālikaḷ toṅkaviṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. maṉiviḷakku erintukoṇ ṭiruntatu. uḷuttam paruppuṭaṉ kūṭṭic camitta poṅkalōṭu viruntu naṭipeṟṟuk koṇṭiruntatu. cantiraṉi urōkiṇi kūṭiya nallōriyil mutiya maṅkala makaḷir maṇamakaḷi nīrāṭṭutaṟkuriya nīri mukantutara, makkaḷip peṟṟa maṅkala makaḷir nālvar kūṭiniṉṟu, kaṟpiṉiṉṟum vaḻuvātu naṉṟāya pala pēṟukaḷiyum tantu niṉṉi eytiya kaṇavaṉi virumpip pēṇum viruppatti uṭiyi ākuka, eṉṟu vāḻtti, annīril nelliyum mañcaḷiyum kalantu maṇamakaḷi nīrāṭṭiṉar (86).
iṅṅaṉam maṅkala makaḷir maṇamakaḷi nīrāṭṭuvatu akkālattil mikacciṟanta caṭaṅkāyk karutappaṭṭatu. ceyyuḷ 136 il vēṟoruvakit tirumaṇam kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
iṟicciyuṭaṉ kūṭṭiyākkiya ney mikunta veṇ cōṟṟi maṇattiṟku vantavar uṇṭaṉar. tiṅkaḷ urōkiṇiyuṭaṉ kūṭiya nallōriyil maṇamaṉi aḻaku ceyyappaṭṭatu. kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭu naṭi peṟṟatu. muṇa muḻavuṭaṉ periya muracam olittatu. vāḻvaracikaḷ maṇamakaḷukku maṇa nīrāṭṭiṉar. vāki iliyi arukiṉ kiḻaṅkiṭattuḷḷa arumpuṭaṉ cērak kaṭṭiya veḷḷiya nūlic cūṭṭi maṇamakkaḷit tūya uṭiyāl poliyac ceytaṉar. maṇamakaḷ pala aṇikaḷi aṇintiruntāḷ. mēkam olittatupōla maṇa oli olittatu. uṟaviṉar aṉivarum kūṭi maṇamakaḷi maṇamakaṉukku maṉiviyākat tantaṉar.
ivviraṇṭu tirumaṇaṅkaḷilum āriyar cār puṭiyatu etuvum illi-eri vaḷarttal illi. tīvalam varutal illi. taṭciṇi peṟap purōkitaṉ illi. ivi muṟṟum tamiḻarkkē uriya tirumaṇa muṟikaḷākum. eṉṟu varalāṟṟup pērāciriyar tiru. pi. ṭi. cīnivāca ayyaṅkār kūṟiyuḷḷatu kāṇka.6
camayac ceytikaḷ: kūṭali yaṭutta tirupparaṅkuṉṟattil murukaṉ tirukkōyil iruntatu (59). aṅku ōyvillāmal viḻākkaḷ naṭantapaṭi iruntaṉa (149) alivāy eṉpatu kaṭaṟkari ūr. aṅku aḻakiya maṇiviḷakku oṉṟu oḷi tantukoṇṭiruntatu. aṅkum murukaṉ kōvil iruntatu (266). maturiyil maliyi otta uyarnta kōvil iruntatu (290). civaṉum tirumālum, iruperunteyvaṅkaḷ eṉṟu pōṟṟappaṭṭaṉar (360). ovvōr ūrilum akkālattil potiyil (ampalam) iruntatu. ataṉil kantu eṉṉum vaḻipāṭṭukkuriya marattūṇ (iliṅka vaṭivil amintatu) iruntatu. ūr makkaḷ viṭiyaṟkālattil ataṉi vaḻipaṭṭaṉar (287).
caṅkakālat tamiḻar tam piḷḷikaḷukkut tāli aṇivittal vaḻakkam. at tāliyil kāttaṟ kaṭavuḷāṉa tirumāliṉ aintu paṭikaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷum poṟikkap peṟṟiruntatu. atu aimpaṭit tāli eṉavum peyar peṟṟatu. ataṉi āṇ makaṉum peṇ makaḷum aṇintiruntaṉar (54).
uyarnta malikaḷmītu kārttiki viḻāviṟku viḷakkukaḷi iṭutal caṅkakāla vaḻakkam eṉṟu pālipāṭiya peruṅkaṭuṅkō eṉṟa cēramaṉṉaṉ kūṟiyuḷḷāṉ. cēranāṭu malināṭu. iṉṟu tiruvaṇṇāmali ucciyil iṭappaṭuvatu pōlac cēranāṭṭu malikaḷiṉ ucciyil periya viḷaṅkukaḷ iṭappeṟṟaṉa pōlum!
uṟiyūri yaṭutta kāviriyiṉ kariyilirunta cōliyil paṅkuṉi viḻā naṭipeṟṟatu (137). koṅku nāṭṭiṉar maṇiyiṉi iṭiyil kaṭṭikkoṇṭu uḷḷi viḻāviṉpōtu teruvil āṭuvar (368). akkālat tamiḻakattil uṇṇāmiyāl vāṭiya vayiṟṟiṉiyum, nīrāṭāta uṭaliyum uṭiya tavattiṉar (camaṇattuṟavikaḷ) iruntaṉar (123).
nākarikam: caṅkakāla makaḷir pantu eṟital, kaḻaṅkāṭutal mutaliya āṭṭaṅkaḷil ciṟantiruntaṉar (17, 66). cēravēntaratu cuḷḷi eṉṟa āṟṟil yavaṉar kappal poṉṉuṭaṉ vantu miḷakoṭu mīṇṭatu (148). ūlakam nakarntālotta accam tōṉṟum kappal (255) akkālattil iruntatu.
caṅkakālat tamiḻar icik kaliyil vallavar eṉpatip pala pāṭalkaḷ uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa (102, 186, 346, 355). tiṉippuṉam kāvalkātta peṇ, kuṟiñcip paṇṇip pāṭikkoṇ ṭiruntāḷ. appōtu tiṉik katirit tutikkiyil piṭittirunta iḷiya āṇ yāṉi iciyil īṭupaṭṭut tiṉik katiri uṇṇāmalum, niṉṟa niliyiliruntu akalāmalum uṟaṅkiviṭṭatu eṉṉum ceyti kuṟamakaḷiṉ icic ciṟappi iṉitu viḷakkukiṟataṉṟō? (102). tiṉippuṉam kātta kuṟamakaḷir tiṉiyi uṇṇavarum kiḷi mutaliya paṟavikaḷi ōṭṭuvataṟkup pāṭal pāṭiṉar. atu kiḷikaṭi pāṭal eṉappaṭṭatu (118).
caṅkakāla naṭaṉamakaḷ viṟali eṉappaṭṭāḷ; uḷḷak kuṟippup puṟattil veḷippaṭumpaṭi viṟalpaṭa naṭittatāl viṟalieṉap peyar peṟṟāḷ. avaḷ naṭikkum pōtu muḻavu koṭṭuvōṉ ivaḷukkup piṉpuṟam niṟpatu vaḻakkam. ūril naṭipeṟum viḻākkaḷil viṟali naṭippatu vaḻakkam (352).
paṇpāṭu: akanāṉūṟṟup pāṭalkaḷ iṉpam paṟṟiyaṉa āyiṉum, tamiḻar paṇpāṭṭi viḷakkum uyarnta karuttukkaḷi āṅkāṅkuk koṇṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ cilavaṟṟi iṅkē kāṇpōm;
(1)  cāṉṟōr mikka kātal koṇṭavarāyiṉum paḻiyuṭaṉ kūṭivarum iṉpattiṉi virumpār (112).
(2) pāvaneṟiyiṟ cellāta vāḻkkiyum, piṟaṉ maṉi  vāyilil ceṉṟu nillāta mēmpāṭum ākiya iraṇṭum poruḷiṉāl ākum (155).
(3) uḷḷam virumpiya vaḻiyil ataṉic cella viṭāmal, virumpiya poruḷiṉ naṉmi tīmikaḷi ārāyntu, uḷḷatti nallataṉvaḻic celuttuvatē periyōr oḻukkamākum (287).
(4) irappavaratu ēntum ki niṟiyumpaṭi makiḻcci yōṭu virintuvantu putiya poruḷkaḷit tantu makiḻuvataṟkē ariya celvatti īṭṭavēṇṭum (389).
ittakiya uyarnta paṇpāṭuṭiya tamiḻ makkaḷ vāḻnta kālamē caṅka kālam.
purāṇa itikācak katikaḷ : kaṇṇaṉ yamuṉit tuṟiyil nīrāṭiya kōpiyar āṭikaḷik kavarntami. murukaṉ cūraṉik koṉṟami, irāmaṉ kōṭikkariyil paṟavikaḷ oliyi aṭakkiṉami, paracurāmaṉ maṉṉar marapi aḻittami pōṉṟa cila kati nikaḻccikaḷ akanāṉūṟṟup pāṭalkaḷil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa (59, 70, 220 mutaliyaṉa).
vaṭacoṟkaḷ: innūṟ pāṭalkaḷil vaṭacoṟkaḷ tamiḻ uruppeṟṟum peṟāmalum vaḻaṅkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. niti (60), irāmaṉ (70), vatuvi (86), aramiyam (124), vīti (147), kalāpam (152), ciramam (181), cimayam (208), pati (299) kēyam (333), ivipōṉṟa cila coṟkaḷē innūlil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
14. kalittoki
kalittokiyiṉ putumikaḷ
1. ainkuṟunūṟu, kuṟuntoki, naṟṟiṇi, akanāṉūṟu ākiya nāṉku nūlkaḷum akavaṟpālil aminta akapporuḷ ceyyuṭkaḷik koṇṭavi. mēlum avi aṉittum akattiṇi paṟṟiya ceytikaḷiyē koṇṭavi; tol kāppiyaruṭiya kaḷaviyalukkum, kaṟpiyalukkum perumpālum ilakkiyamāka amintavi. kalittokip pāṭalkaḷ kalippāvil amintavi. ivaṟṟuḷ cila 80 aṭi nīḷamum uṭiyavi; tolkāppiyar poruḷiyalil kūṟiyuḷḷa vitikaṭku ilakkiyamāka amintavi. ataṉāl kikkiḷi, peruntiṇi paṟṟiya pāṭalkaḷum, maṭalēṟutal paṟṟiya pāṭalkaḷum, iḻintōr kātal paṟṟiya pāṭalkaḷum mikutiyāka iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
2. naṟṟiṇi mutaliya akanūlkaḷil taliviyiṉ mukam, kūntal, uṭalvaḷam, avaḷatu nalam mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkut tamiḻ nāṭṭup pakutikaḷ, nakaraṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉavē uvamikaḷāy vantuḷḷaṉa. kalittokiyil vantuḷḷa uvamikaḷuḷ perumpālāṉavi, naṟṟiṇi mutaliya nūlkaḷil kāṇap peṟāta itikāca purāṇak katikaḷākavē amintuḷḷaṉa.
3. naṟṟiṇi mutaliya pāṭalkaḷiṉ naṭi miṭukkuṭiyatu. kalittokip pāṭalkaḷiṉ naṭi miṭukkut taḷarntatu.
4. naṟṟiṇi mutaliya nūṟpākkaḷil pēraracar, ciṟṟaracar nāṭukaḷum, nakaraṅkaḷum, malikaḷum, āṟukaḷum iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. āyiṉ kalittokip pākkaḷiṟ pāṇṭiyaṉ (57), kūṭal (66, 91, 92), viyi (97), potiyil (57) maṭṭumē kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiyarkaḷō avartam ūrkaḷō, piṟavō kuṟikkappaṭavilli. itu kavaṉikkattakum ceytiyākum.
5. ovvoru kaliyilum orē tuṟi aminta pala pāṭalkaḷ cuviyaṟṟa niliyil pāṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cāṉṟāka, talivaṉ maṭalūrtal paṟṟi nāṉku pāṭalkaḷum (136-139), kāmaveṟi koṇṭa talivi pitaṟṟal paṟṟi āṟu pāṭalkaḷum (143-148) vantuḷḷaṉa.
6. cila ceyyuṭkaḷ tōḻi-talivi uriyāṭal (60), talivi-talivaṉ uriyāṭal (64), tōḻi-talivaṉ uriyāṭal (61) muṟiyil amintuḷḷaṉa.
7. piṟa akanūlkaḷil kāṇappeṟāta niṉṟītti(93); pōcītti (93), pāṭiṉatti (130), iḵtottaṉ (84) pōṉṟa coṟkaḷ itil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
8. naṟṟiṇi mutaliya nūlkaḷil kāmakkiḻatti pēcuvatākac ceyyuḷ iṭam peṟavilli. āyiṉ, innūlil cila ceyyuṭkaḷ (69, 72, 73) iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
9. kalippākkaḷil varum perumpālarāṉa talivar taliviyar viṉival pāṅkar ātalāl talivi, talivaṉi ēṭā eṉṟum, talivaṉ taliviyi ēṭi eṉṟum aḻippatik kāṇkiṟōm (87, 90).
10. marutak kaliyil vantuḷḷa kūṉi-kuṟaḷaṉ kātal uriyāṭal (94) naṟṟiṇi mutaliya nūlkaḷil kāṇumāṟu illi.
11. viyāḻaṉum veḷḷiyum vaṭamoḻiyil aṟanūlkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷaṉar. annūlkaḷiṉpaṭi nāṭāṇṭavaṉē eṉṟu talivaṉ oru ceyyuḷil (99) aḻikkappaṭukiṟāṉ. ittakiya ceyti vēṟu toki nūlkaḷil illi eṉpatu kavaṉikkattakum.
12. civaperumāṉ muppuram erittatu (1, 150), turiyōtaṉaṉ pāṇṭavari arakku māḷikiyil kolla muyaṉṟami (25), murukaṉ-cūrapatumaṉ pōr (27), irāvaṇaṉ kayili maliyip peyarttatu (38), vīmaṉ turiyōtaṉaṉit tuṭiyil aṭittatu (52), kaṇṇaṉ kamcaṉāl ēvappaṭṭa mallari aḻittatu (134), vīmaṉ tuccātaṉaṉ neñcip piḷantatu (101) civaṉ emaṉi utittami (101, acuvattāmaṉ taṉ tantiyik koṉṟa cikaṇṭiyik koṉṟami (101), kaṇṇaṉ kamcaṉāl ēvappaṭṭa kutiri uruvattil vanta acuraṉik koṉṟami (103), kaṇṇaṉ taṉ āḻiyāṉ maṟitta cūriyaṉi mīṭṭami (104), kaṉṉaṉ cūriyaṉukku makaṉ (108), ūrvaci tilōttami paṟṟiya ceyti (109), yayāti aracaṉ kati (139), civaṉ taṉ caṭiyil kaṅkiyi maṟittami (150). ivaṟṟuḷ civaṉ muppuram erittamiyum, murukaṉ cūrapatumaṉi aḻittamiyum piṟa nūlkaḷil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ēṉiyavi mutaṉmutalāka innūliltām kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa eṉpatu kavaṉikkat takum. ivi aṉittum innūliṟkē uriya putumikaḷākum.
kalittoki āciriyar
kalittoki eṉpatu kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu uṭpaṭa 153 kalippākkaḷik koṇṭatākum. ivi pāli; kuṟiñci, marutam, mulli, neytal eṉṉum oḻukkavakiyil amin tuḷḷaṉa. pālittiṇi paṟṟi 35 ceyyuṭkaḷum, kuṟiñcittiṇi paṟṟi 29 ceyyuṭkaḷum, marutattiṇi paṟṟi 35 pākkaḷum, mullittiṇi paṟṟi 17 pāṭalkaḷum, neytal tiṇipaṟṟi 37 pāṭalkaḷum itaṉ kaṇ amintuḷḷaṉa.
ēṟattāḻak ki. pi. 14 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup piṟpaṭṭavar eṉak karutattakum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar eṉṟa uriyāciriyar innūlukku aḻakiya uri eḻutiyuḷḷār. avar kaṭavuḷ vāḻttup pakutiyil, ātalāl īṇṭup pālittiṇiyiyum tiṇiyāka āciriyar nallantuvaṉār kōttār eṉṟu kūṟuka, eṉṟum, nūliṉ iṟutiyil, mulli, kuṟiñci, marutam eṉac colliya muṟiyāl collavum paṭumē eṉpuḻic collāta muṟiyāl collavum paṭum eṉṟaliṉ, ittoki yip pāli, kuṟiñci, marutam, mulli, neytal eṉa immuṟiyē kōttār nallantuvaṉār, eṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷār. avarē neytaṟkali 25 ām ceyyuḷ uriyil, colleccamum kuṟippeccamumākat tam pēraṟivu tōṉṟa āciriyar nallantuvaṉār ceyyuḷ ceytār, eṉpatiyum kuṟittuḷḷār.
ik kūṟṟukkaḷi nōkka, nallantuvaṉār neytaṟ kaliyi maṭṭum pāṭiṉavar eṉpatum, attuṭaṉ tam kālattil irunta piṟa kalippākkaḷic cērttu teḷivupaṭuttiyavar eṉpatum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar karuttātal teḷivākum. āyiṉ, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar tamatu uriyil piṟa kalikaḷi iyaṟṟiya āciriyar peyarkaḷic cuṭṭavilli. itaṉāl avar kālattilēyē piṟa kalikaḷip pāṭiya āciriyarkaḷip paṟṟi oṉṟum teriyavilli eṉpatu teḷivākum.
kalittokiyi mutalil veḷiyiṭṭa tiru. ci. vi. tāmōtaram piḷḷi avarkaḷ, innūl iyaṟṟiyōr oruvarē eṉak karutiṉar. ataṉpiṉ,
peruṅkaṭuṅkōṉ pāli kapilaṉ kuṟiñci
marutaṉiḷa nākaṉ marutam-aruñcōḻaṉ
nallurut tiraṉmulli nallan tuvaṉeytal
kalvivalār kaṇṭa kali.
eṉṉum veṇpā veḷippaṭṭatu. atu mutal, aintu kaliyum pulavar aivarāl pāṭappaṭṭavi eṉat tamiḻaṟiñar karutalāyiṉar. ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakat tamiḻārāyccit tuṟip pulavarāy irunta tiru K. N. civarācap piḷḷi avarkaḷ, kalittoki āciriyar oruvarē eṉa muṭivu kaṭṭiṉar.
aivar allar-kāraṇaṅkaḷ:
(1) kalittoki āciriyar aivarē eṉpataṟku mēṟkāṭṭiya veṇpāvit tavira vēṟu cāṉṟu illi. eṉavē, avveṇpā nampattakkatā eṉpati mutaṟkaṇ ārāytal vēṇṭum.
1.	 pālik kaliyip pāṭiyavar	-	peruṅkaṭuṅkōṉ		
2. kuṟiñcik     				 -	kapilar				
3. marutak 	        			-	marutaṉ iḷanākaṉār			
4. mullik     			-	cōḻaṉ nalluruttiraṉ		
5. neytaṟ      		 		-	nallantuvaṉār			
eṉpatu avveṇpāvil uḷḷa ceyti. ēṉiya toki nūlkaḷil pālittiṇi paṟṟip pala ceyyuṭkaḷik kaṭuṅkōṉ pāṭiyiruttalālum, kuṟiñcit tiṇi paṟṟip pala ceyyuṭkaḷik kapilar pāṭiyiruttalālum, kalikaḷi ivarkaḷ pāṭiṉārkaḷ eṉak koḷḷiṉum, ēṉiya mūṉṟiyum piṟa pulavar mūvarum pāṭat takuti vāyntavar eṉpataṟkuriya cāṉṟu ēṉit toki nūlkaḷil kāṇak kūṭavilli. marutaṉ iḷanākaṉār piṟa nūlkaḷil pāṭiyavaṉavākak kāṇappaṭum 39 ceyyuṭkaḷil pālipaṟṟi 17m, kuṟiñci paṟṟi 9m, mullipaṟṟi 5m, neytalpaṟṟi 5m, marutampaṟṟi mūṉṟumē uḷḷaṉa. ivar marutakkaliyip pāṭiṉār eṉpatu poruntuvatāka uḷḷatā? cōḻaṉ nalluruttiraṉ puṟanāṉūṟṟil oru ceyyuḷē (190) pāṭiyatākat terikiṟatu. avaṉ pāṭiya pā oṉṟum vēṟu toki nūlkaḷil illi. avaṉ mullik kaliyip pāṭiṉāṉ eṉa avveṇpā kūṟukiṟatu. nallantuvaṉār neytaṟṟiṇiyil oru ceyyuḷum ceytilar. avar neytaṟkaliyip pāṭiyatāka veṇpā viḷampukiṟatu.1
(2) mullik kaliyil kūṟappaṭum mulli nila makkaḷ pāṇṭiya nāṭṭavarākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ pāṇṭiyaṉi vāḻttuvatākac ceyyuḷ kūṟukiṟatu. cōḻa vēntaṉ taṉatu nāṭṭu mulli nila makkaḷip pāṭātatu viyappē aṉṟō? cōḻa aracaṉ taṉatu nāṭṭu makkaḷip paṟṟip pāṭāmal pāṇṭiya nāṭṭu mullinila makkaḷip paṟṟi maṭṭum taṉatu mullik kaliyil pāṭiṉāṉ eṉṉal caṟṟum poruntātu. mulli nilam pāṇṭiyarkkē urimiyuṭiyatum aṉṟu. pāṇṭiyaṉi vāḻttip paravum oru kuṟippiṭṭa mulli nilattārip paṟṟic cōḻavēntaṉ nūl pāṭiṉāṉ eṉpatu aṟiviṟkum anupavattiṟkum caṟṟum poruntāta tākum.
(8) neytal tiṇiyip paṟṟi oru ciṟu ceyyuḷum ēṉiya toki nūlkaḷuṭpāṭiyirāta oruvar, neytaṟ kaliyip pāṭiṉār eṉpatu, karukkoḷḷātu makappēṟu tōṉṟiṉāṟ pōlākum aṉṟō?
(4) kalittoki muḻumiyum naṉku ārāyiṉ, ceyyuṭkaḷ orē āciriyar iyaṟṟiyaṉa eṉpatu pulaṉām. itaṉi oppukkoḷḷa maṉamaṟṟavar ivveṇpāviṉi veḷikkiḷampa viṭṭaṉar. ivveṇpā entak kalit toki ēṭṭilum illi; vēṟu nūl ēṭṭup paṭikaḷilum illi; māyamāy vantatākum: itaṉi naṉku eṇṇātu, pulavarkaḷ kalit tokiyi aivar pāṭiyatākak koṇṭu viṭṭaṉar.2 itu muṟṟum ātāramaṟṟa-ikkālap pulavar evarō kaṭṭiviṭṭa veṇpā ākum. eṉpatē pērāciriyar tiru e. viyāpurippiḷḷi, tiru vittuvāṉ vē. vēṅkaṭarājalu reṭṭiyār mutaliyōr karuttumākum.
(5) tirunelvēli-cuvarṇam piḷḷi avarkaḷ tamakkuk kiṭittatākak  kūṟiya ūci muṟi eṉṉum nūlit tamiḻp pērāciriyar tiru kā. namaccivāya mutaliyār avarkaḷ poruḷ koṭuttu vāṅki ārāyntārkaḷ; atu pōli nūl-poruḷukkāka ikkālap pulavar pāṭiya poynnūl-eṉpati aṟintu kiḻitteṟintārkaḷ. iṅṅaṉam poruḷukkākavum viḷiyāṭṭākavum palar tāmē kaṭṭiya pākkaḷip paḻam pāṭalkaḷ eṉakkūṟi ēmāṟṟal uṇṭu. ivveṇpāviṉ paṭippu attakiyōruḷ oruvar ceyalāka irukkalām eṉak kōṭalē poruntum.
kalittokip pākkaḷiṉ naṭi, moḻi amippu, col amippu, nūl muḻumiyum purāṇa itikāca katikaḷ viravik kiṭattal, piṟa akapporuḷ nūlkaḷil kuṟikkappeṟṟa ciṟṟaracar pēraracar peyarkaḷuḷ oṉṟēṉum kuṟikkappaṭāmi mutaliyavi, innūl kapilar, paraṇar mutaliya pulavarkaḷukkup piṟpaṭṭa oruvarāl, piṟkālattil (perumpālum caṅka iṟutik kālattil) pāṭappaṭṭa nūlāka iruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu niṉikka iṭam tarukiṉṟaṉa. ippāṭalkaḷi aivarō, palarō pāṭiṉar eṉpati uṇarttum cāṉṟu ippākkaḷiṉ naṭiyil illi.3
kalittokiyiṉ kālam
1. kalittokic ceyyuṭkaḷ perumpālum poruḷiyal cūttiraṅkaḷukku ilakkiyamāy amintavi: kikkiḷi, peruntiṇic ceyyuṭkaḷi uṭiyavi.
2. 	miyiṉ matiyiṉ viḷaṅku mukattāri
	pavvik koḷalum aṟaṉeṉak kaṇṭaṉṟu.
		-kuṟiñcikkali-26.
peṇṭiri vuvikkōṭal irākkata maṇam eṉappaṭum. irākkata maṇattit tolkāppiyar kūṟiṟṟilar. peruntiṇi -ovvāta kāmam eṉat tolkāppiyar kūṟiṉarē aṉṟi, irākkata maṇamuṟiyākiya kuvik kōṭalit tamiḻ makkaḷ kiyāṇṭaṉar eṉṟu kūṟiṟṟilar. irākkata maṇam aṟaṉ eṉat tolkāppiyarō caṅkanūṟ pulavarō yāṇṭum kūṟiṟṟilar. āriyaruṭiya eṇ vaki maṇaṅkaḷuḷ irākkata maṇam oṉṟākum. tamiḻarkku immaṇam uṇṭeṉak kūṟac caṅkilakkiyac cāṉṟilli. aṭiyōr talivarākak koḷḷiṉum, tamiḻakattuḷ aṭippaṭṭa paḻakkattukku māṟākac caṅkattuc cāṉṟōr ceyyuḷ ceyyār. ākavē, irākkata maṇam aṟaṉ eṉakkūṟum icceyyuḷum itupōṉṟa kalicceyyuṭkaḷum kaṭiccaṅkap pulavarukkup piṟpaṭṭa kālattaṉavākum eṉpatik kūṟavum vēṇṭumō?
2. kuṟiñcikkalic ceyyuḷ oṉṟil talivi, tōḻi, nī teruvil pōvārkkellām iraṅkutal, vāraṇa vāciyil (kāci) piṟar varuttam tam varuttamākak karutum aruḷuṭiyār ceyali ottuḷḷatē. (24) eṉak kūṟiyuḷḷatu karutaṟpālatu. ēṉiya toki nūlkaḷuḷ, icceyti kāṇappaṭṭilatu; vāraṇavāci (kāci) nakara māntar ceyti enta aḷavu tamiḻakattil paravi iruntāl, ataṉit talimakaḷ colvatākap pulavar pāṭiyiruppar eṉpatu cintikkat takkatu. ēṉiya paḻam pāṭalkaḷil kāṇappeṟāta  iv vaṭanāṭṭuc ceyti caṅkakālattiṉ iṟutiyil, allatu piṟpaṭṭa kālattil tamiḻakattārāl aṟiyappaṭṭatātal vēṇṭum.
4. kuṟiñcik kaliyuḷ aintiṇi paṟṟik kūṟaṟkuriya ariya ceytikaḷi, ēṉic cāṉṟōrip piṉpaṟṟik kūṟutali oḻittu avarāl kaṉavilum karutappaṭātaviyum, collap paṭātaviyumāṉa kikkiḷi, peruntiṇic ceytikaḷiyum, iḻintōr kaḷaviṉiyum, marutakkaliyuḷ kūṉum kuṟaḷum uṟaḻntu kūṟalum, puṇartalum (29) pōṉṟa ceytikaḷiyum, tēḷ koṭṭi ēṟum viṣam pōlak kāmam talikkēṟu kiṟatō? eṉṟāṟpōlak kūṟappaṭum mullikkalic ceyti kaḷiyum, ivipōṉṟē ēṉiya kalikaḷilum veṟukkat takka-paṇṭip pulavar neṟikku māṟākak kūṟappaṭum ceytikaḷiyum, accaṅkakālap pulavarōṭu irunta cāṉṟōr pāṭiyirār eṉpatu aṟivum naṭuvu nilimiyum uṭiyār naṉkaṟital kūṭumaṉṟō? ivaṟṟukku ilakkaṇam tol kāppiyattuḷ kāṇappṭiṉum, ivaṟṟip piṟkālattup pulavar pāṭiṉar eṉak kōṭalē aṟivukkum ārāyccikkum poruttamākat terikiṟatu. aṟiñar mēlum āyvārāka.
5. kāmaṉ āriyak kaṭavuḷ avaṉ, kāmaṉār eṉavum, cāmaṉārkku mūtta kāmaṉār eṉavum marutak kaliyuḷ (ce-29) kūṟappaṭṭāṟ pōlap piṟa toki nūlkaḷuḷ kūṟappaṭṭilaṉ.
6. kalittoki muḻumiyilum enta aracaṉ peyarum cuṭṭappaṭavilli. mūvēntaruḷ pāṇṭiyaṉē cuṭṭappaṭu kiṉṟāṉ.  viyi, kūṭal iviyē cuṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ēṉiya toki nūlkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭum vaḷḷalkaḷō, pulavarkaḷō cuṭṭappaṭavilli. mēṟkōḷ kāṭṭattakum iṭaṅkaḷil vāraṇavācip patam pōṉṟa vaṭanāṭṭuc ceytikaḷum, uvamikaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟil purāṇak katikaḷum, teyvaṅkaḷumē kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ēṉiya akappāṭṭukkaḷil perumpālāṉa ceyyuṭkaḷil vantuḷḷa tamiḻp pulavar peyarkaḷ, vaḷḷalkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ, aracarkaḷ peyarkaḷ, pōrkaḷ mutaliyaṉa pōṉṟa tamiḻakac ceytikaḷuḷ oṉṟēṉum 149 ceyyuṭkaḷik koṇṭa kalittokiyil payiṉṟu varāmi naṉku kavaṉittaṟkuriyatu. ittakiya kāraṇam koṇṭē kalittokic
kapilar, paraṇar, nakkīrar pōṉṟa caṅkattuc cāṉṟōr akattiṇi paṟṟiya pāṭalkaḷiyē pāṭiyuḷḷaṉar. tolkāppiyap poruḷiyal vitikaṭkuriya pākkaḷip pāṭavilli. ak kuṟiyi nīkkavē appulavarkaṭkup piṉpu tōṉṟiya pulavar oruvar innūlip pāṭiyiruttal kūṭum. innūlil pāṇṭiyanāṭu oṉṟē kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. pāṇṭiyaṉē pārāṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷāṉ. eṉavē, kalittoki pāṭiya pulavar pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉarāyiruttal kūṭum.
kalittokiyil pāṇṭiyaṉ, avaṉ talinakarāṉa maturi, viyi āṟu, potiyilmali, avaṉatu nērmiyāṉa āṭci eṉpaṉa iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟuṭaṉ pāṇṭiyar vaḷartta tamiḻc caṅkam paṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷum kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. muṉpu kuṟippiṭṭatupōl cēra cōḻar paṟṟiyō, pukaḻpeṟṟa kuṟunila maṉṉarip paṟṟiyō oru ciṟu kuṟippum innūlil iṭam peṟavilli. ivi aṉittiyum naṭuvu niliyiliruntu nōkka, piṟa pulavarkaḷ pāṭātu viṭṭa poruḷiyal vitikaṭkēṟṟa pāṭalkaḷik koṇṭa ikkalit toki eṉṉum nūl, caṅka kālattiṉ iṟutiyil-kaḷappirar paṭiyeṭuppukkuc ciṟitu muṟpaṭṭa kālattil-pāṇṭiyaṉ mītum avaṉatu nāṭṭiṉmītum aḷavaṟṟa paṟṟukkoṇṭa pāṇṭiya nāṭṭup pulavar oruvarāl pāṭappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu koḷvatu mikavum poruttamākum  .4 ippulavar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar tam uriyil kuṟippiṭṭa āciriyar nallantuvaṉār āyiṉ, in nallantuvaṉār piṟa nūlkaḷiluḷḷa pāṭalkaḷip pāṭiya nallantuvaṉāriṉum vēṟupaṭṭavar eṉak koḷḷalē poruttamuṭiyatu. eṉavē, kalittokiyiṉ kālam caṅka iṟutikkālam, aḵtāvatu, ēṟattāḻak ki. pi. 300 eṉṉalām.
nūṟceytikaḷ cila
ēṟu taḻuval: kāṭum kāṭu cārnta iṭamum mulli eṉappaṭum. āyar eṉpavar mullinila makkaḷ, piṭavam, kōṭal, kāyā, koṉṟi, veṭci, taḷavam, mulli, kuruntam, pāṅkar mutaliya malarkaḷ mulli nilattiṟkuriyavi. ivi taṉittaṉiyē kaṭṭappaṭṭum, kalaviyākak kaṭṭappaṭṭum āyarāl aṇiyappeṟum (101, 103).
mullikkaliyuḷ āyar ēṟutaḻuval paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ aḻakuṟa amintuḷḷaṉa. āyar civaperumāṉ maḻuvippōla erutukaḷiṉ kompukaḷic cīvuvar (101); erutukaḷuḷ palatēvaṉippōṉṟa veṇṇiṟam koṇṭavi cila; kaṇṇaṉippōlak kariyavi cila; civaṉip pōlac cemmiyum karumiyum kalantavi cila; murukaṉippōlac civantavi cila (105); intiraṉippōl pala puḷḷikaḷi uṭiyavi cila; murukaṉatu veḷḷiya tukilippōla veḷḷiya kālkaḷiyuṭiyavi cila; emaṉippōṉṟa valimiyuṭiyavi cilavākum (106). ivi aṉittum toḻuvattil viṭappaṭum.
mullinila iḷiñarkaḷ attoḻuvil pāyvataṟku muṉpu nīrttuṟiyilum, ālamarattiṉ kīḻum, māmarattiṉ kīḻum uṟiyum teyvaṅkaḷi vaḻipaṭuvar (102, 103). kaṉṉippeṇkaḷ paraṇmītu niṉṟu vēṭikki pārppar (102 103).
vīrarkaḷ toḻuvattuḷ pāyntavuṭaṉ ēṟukaḷit taḻuvi aṭakka muṟpaṭuvar. āvi cila vīrarkaḷik kuttic cāykkum; cilar kuṭalkaḷi veḷiyē taḷḷac ceyyum; kuṭalkaḷ cila erutukaḷiṉ kompukaḷil cuṟṟik koḷvatum uṇṭu. ēṟukaḷi aṭakkum vīrarkaḷ āyarālum āycciyarālum pārāṭṭappaṭuvar. iṟutiyil vīrarum maṅkiyarum ūr maṉṟattil kikōttuk kuravikkūttu āṭuvar appoḻutu āycciyar cila pāṭalkaḷip pāṭi āṭuvar (101, 104;) iṟutiyil aracaṉi vāḻvikkumpaṭi tirumāli vēṇṭuvar (103, 106).
ēṟu taḻuvalil īṭupaṭāta iḷiñaṉi āyarmakaḷ kaṇavaṉāka ēlāḷ (103). āyaruḷ āṭumāṭukaḷi mēyttavar āṭṭiṭiyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar; pulliṉattār allatu kuṟumpar eṉavum paṭṭaṉar. pacu iṭiyar kōiṉattār eṉṟum nalliṉattār eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭaṉar (107, 113.).
taliviyiṉ tantiyōṭu avaḷ tamiyaṉmārum uṭaṉiruntu avaḷatu tirumaṇattiṟku icivu taruvar (107).
viṉivala pāṅkaṉāṉa talivaṉ, taliviyiṉ tamiyaṉukku uṇavu koṇṭu celvāṉ; pacukkūṭṭat tilirukkum taliviyiṉ tantikkuk kaṟavikkalam (pāl kaṟakkum pāttiram) koṇṭu celvāṉ; kaṉṟukaḷi mēyppāṉ (108); tirumāli eṇṇit taliyiṉālē vaṇaṅki kiyāl toṭṭuc cūḷ urippāṉ (168).
āyar tam vīṭṭic cemmaṇṇāl alaṅkarittut tariyil maṇalip parappuvar; peṇ erumiyiṉ kompit teyvamāka vittu vaḻipaṭṭut tirumaṇam ceyvar. at tirumaṇam perumaṇam eṉappaṭum (113).
tamiḻc caṅkam: iḷavēṉiṟ kālattil cāṉṟōr nāviṟ piṟanta kavikaḷiṉ putumiyi maturi makkaḷ koṇṭāṭuvar eṉṟu oru ceyyuḷ (35) kūṟukiṟatu. itaṉi nōkka, iḷavēṉil eṉṉum iṉpantarum kālattiltāṉ, pulavarkaḷ putiya ceyyuṭkaḷi iyaṟṟik koṇṭu maturiyil kūṭiṉar eṉṟu niṉippatu poruttamākum.
viyiyāṟu maturiyic cūḻntu vantatu. attōṟṟam nilamakaḷ oru pūmāliyic cūṭiyirunta taṉmiyi ottiruntatu. kārkālam mutirntamiyāl kottuk kottāka malarkaḷ viyi nīril paṭintu vantaṉa. viyi nīr kōṭṭi matilmītu mōtik koṇṭiruntatu. av viyi pulavarāl pārāṭṭappaṭṭatu (67). pulavarkaḷ tam cevikaḷi vayalākavum, tamakku muṟpaṭṭa cāṉṟōr kūṟiya ceyyuṭkaḷ tam colli vaḷarkkum nīrākavum koṇṭu, tamatu aṟivuṭiya nāvākiya kalappiyāl uḻutu uṇpavar. attakiya pulavar perumakkaḷiṉ putiya kavikaḷip pāṇṭiyaṉ koḷḷi koṇṭu makiḻum iyalpuṭiyavaṉ (68). ik kūṟṟukkaḷi nōkka, maturiyil pulavarkaḷ mikkiruntamiyum, pāṇṭiyar avar ceyyuṭkaḷik kēṭṭu anuvavittamiyum, appulavar perumakkaḷ putiya kavikaḷ iyaṟṟiṉamiyum iṉitu pulappaṭukiṉṟaṉa allavā?
kuṟiñci nilat talivaṉ taṉṉi nāṭi vanta pulavar kaḷukkut tērkaḷiyum, kaḷiṟukaḷiyum koṭuttāṉ eṉṟu oru ceyyuḷ kūṟukiṟatu (50). kalittokip pākkaḷ cila, pāṇṭiyaṉiyē talivaṉākak koṇṭu pāṭappaṭṭiruppa tāl, iṅkuk kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa talivaṉip pāṇṭiyaṉākak karututal poruttamākum. tamiḻkeḻu kūṭal eṉṟum, tamiḻ viyi eṉṟum tamiḻukkum kūṭalukkum, tamiḻukkum viyikkum toṭarpuṭimiyip piṟa nūṟpākkaḷum uṇarttalālum, pāṇṭiyarē caṅkam vittut tamiḻ vaḷarttavar eṉṟu kaḷaviyaluriyum piṟa nūlkaḷum kūṟalālum, kalittokip pākkaḷum avvuṇmiyiyē pulappaṭuttu kiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu iṅku aṟiyattakum.
kalvi: kalvi paṟṟiya ceyti mūṉṟu ceyyuṭkaḷil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. oru talivaṉ meypporuḷ kūṟum nūlkaḷik kūṟavallavarkaḷi vaḻipaṭṭu apporuḷi aṟintavaṉ pōla naṉmakkaḷik kaṇṭāl tōṉṟum maṉa aṭakkam uṭiyavaṉ (47). ōṉṟiyum kallāmal mūttavaṉ aṟivukkaṇ illāta neñcam uṭiyavaṉ (130) pāṭaṅkaḷik kaṟpikkum āciriyaṉ taṉpāl oṉṟum peṟāmal maṉam varuntumpaṭi kaṟṟavaṉ viṭalākātu.  avaṉ taṉ kikporuḷi akkalvip poruḷukkuk kimmāṟakak koṭukkavēṇṭum. tāṉ kaṟṟa kalliyiṭattē tavaṟu ceypavaṉatu kalvip poruḷ nāḷtōṟum tēyum. avaṉum tāṉākat tēyntu viṭuvāṉ (ciṟappiḻappāṉ) (149).
aṇikaḷ: innūlil mikappala aṇikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iḻi, muttuvaṭam, kaṇṇi, mēkali, toṭi, makarakkuḻi, varikkuḻi, kaṉaṅkuḻi, teḷḷaric cilampu, iṭapakkuṟi poṟitta tāyattu, pavaḷa vaṭam, poṉmaṇi vaṭam, cataṅki, vayantakam, valliki, pulliki, kaṉṉa cāmari, teyva utti, kaṇṭiki mutaliyaṉa. ḻurattiyar aṇiyum aṇikaḷ
camayac ceytikaḷ: mukkōl pakavar eṉpavar uṟiyilē taṅkiṉa kamaṇṭalattiyuṭiyavar; ari, ayaṉ, araṉ eṉṉum mūvarum oruvarē eṉpati aṟivikkum aṭiyāḷamāka mukkōlit tōḷilē vittavar; kuṭiyi uṭiyavar; kāṭṭiṭattē pōtali iyalpāṉa oḻukkamākak koṇṭavar. (9); piraṇavatti eppoḻutum niṉittiruppavar (126).
tirumāl, palarāmaṉ, civaṉ, murukaṉ ākiya nālvarum ciṟappuṭik kaṭavuḷarāy matikkappaṭṭaṉar. uyirkaḷip paṭikkum piramaṉ mutalvaṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ (129). tirumāliṉ makaṉāṉa kāmaṉ (109), avaṉ tampi cāmaṉ (26) kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉar. āyar makaḷ teyvattiṟkup pāli vaḻaṅkak kōyilukkup pōvāḷ (109). kuṟap peṇkaḷ nērttik kaṭaṉ ceyvatu vaḻakkam (46). kōvil kaṭavuḷ kaṭinakar eṉappaṭṭatu (84). puttēḷir kōṭṭam eṉṟum peyar peṟṟatu (82). makkaḷ kōvili valam varutalum, ciṟu piḷḷikaḷi valamvarac ceyvittalum vaḻakkam (82).
kaṉṉip peṇkaḷ tittiṅkaḷil nīrāṭi nōṉpiruntu piṟar maṉiyiṉkaṇ aiyamēṟṟup pāṭi aṅkup peṟṟavaṟṟip piṟarkkuk koṭuttaṉar. aṅṅaṉam koṭuppatāl tamakku naṟpayaṉ uṇṭākum eṉṟu nampiṉar (59). itu piṟkālattil mārkaḻi nōṉpu eṉappaṭṭatu.
iruṭikaḷ māliyil aḻali āvuti paṇṇi eḻuppuvar (130). ivarkaḷ maṉiviyarōṭu vāḻntu koṇṭē tavam ceypavar. ivarkaḷ kaṭavuḷar eṉappaṭṭaṉar (93).
ariya tavaṅkaḷik ceytavar pēriṉpatti aṭivar eṉpatu nampappaṭṭatu (30). nūṟ kēḷviyiṉiyuṭiya antaṇar yākam ceytaṉar (36). avarkaḷ māliyil tam camayak kaṭamikaḷit tavaṟātu ceytaṉar (119).
cila uṇmikaḷ: enta nāṭṭu maṉita iṉattiṟkum potuvāṉa pala uṇmikaḷ innūlil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷami inta nūlukkeṉṟu aminta ciṟappiyalpukaḷuḷ ciṟantatu eṉṉalām. avvuṇmikaḷ pōlavum, mukkōṟ pakavar pōṉṟa periyōr kūṟuvaṉa pōlavum, viṉivala pāṅkar kūṟuvaṉa pōlavum innūlil amintuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ cilavaṟṟi iṅkuk kāṇpōm:
1.	celvam niliyillāp poruḷ (8).
2.	vaṟiyavaṉatu iḷami ciṟappi aṭiyātu; koṭikkuṇam illātavaṉuṭiya celvam avaṉic cērntavarip pātukāvātu (10).
3.	yāvarkkum tīṅku ceypavaṉ iṟutiyil uṟaviṉar iṉṟi, naṇpar iṉṟik keṭṭu oḻivāṉ (10).
4.	iḷamiyum kāmamum nāḷ tōṟum kaḻiyum iyalpiṉa (12).
5.	aṟivillātavar tamakku iṟuti uṇṭu eṉpatiyum mūppu uṇṭu eṉpatiyum maṟantu viṭuvar (12).
6.	nērmiyaṟṟa muṟiyil poruḷ tēṭuvārkku, apporuḷ immiyilum maṟumiyilum pakiyākum (14).
7.	muḻumati nāḷ tōṟum tēyvatu pōla iḷamiyum aḻakum tēyum (17).
8.	naṟkuṇamuṭiyavar col tavaṟār (22).
9.	niliyāmiyi uṇarpavar koṭi purivar (32).
10. cōmpalillātavaṉ celvam vaḷarcci aṭiyum (35).
11.	maṉita yākki peṟutaṟku ariyatu (141).
12. poruḷ illātavar nikaḻttum illaṟam iṉpattit tarātu (148).
uvami mutaliyaṉa: kalittokip pākkaḷil uvamikaḷ mikap palavākum. avaṟṟuḷ cila itikāca purāṇa katikaḷik kuṟippavi (101); cila araciyal ceytikaḷ paṟṟiyavi (70); vēṟu cila ulakiyal paṟṟiyavi (69). uruvaka aṇi cila ceyyuṭkaḷil (61, 96, 97, 149) amintuḷḷatu. uḷḷuṟi uvamam pala ceyyuṭkaḷil (39, 123, 132 mutaliyaṉa), amintuḷḷatu. taṟkuṟippēṟṟa aṇiyum iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu (74). iṟiccip poruḷum iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu (40).
mēṟkōḷ:
kalittokip pākkaḷil tirukkuṟaḷ, akanāṉūṟu, cilappatikāram mutaliya caṅka kāla nūlkaḷiṉ karuttukkaḷum toṭarkaḷum coṟkaḷum mikutiyākap payiṉṟu varukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ cilavaṟṟik kīḻē kāṇalām.

7.	mukantāṉē koṭṭik koṭukkum kuṟippu	93

kalittokip pākkaḷil kāṇappaṭum cila karuttukkaḷum, toṭarkaḷum, coṟkaḷum tirikaṭukam, tēvāram mutaliya piṉ nūlkaḷil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ cilavaṟṟik kīḻē kāṇka:
1. kōḷāḷar eṉṉoppār illeṉa nammāṉuḷ
   tāḷāṇmi kūṟum potuvaṉ namakkorunāḷ
  kōḷāḷaṉ ākāmi illi avaṟkaṇṭu
  vēḷāṇmi ceytaṉa kaṇ.	101
tāḷāḷaṉ eṉpāṉ kaṭaṉpaṭā vāḻpavaṉ
vēḷāḷaṉ eṉpāṉ viruntirukka uṇṇātāṉ
kōḷāḷaṉ eṉpāṉ maṟavātāṉ immūvar
kēḷāka vāḻtal iṉitu.	-tirikaṭukam, 13
2aṉṉiyum attaṉum illarāy yāynāṇa
aṉṉiyiyum attaṉiyum aṉṟē nīttāṉ.	-appar tēvāram
vaṭacoṟkaḷ: aiṅkuṟu nūṟu mutaliya akapporuḷ nūlkaḷil vaṭacoṟkaḷ mikak kuṟintu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kalittokip pākkaḷil avaṟṟiviṭac cila coṟkaḷ mikutiyāka iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. innūṟpākkaḷil vaṭanūṟ katikaḷum piṟavum mikutiyāka iṭam peṟṟiruttal muṉpē kuṟikkappaṭṭataṉṟō! kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu nīṅkiya innūṟ pākkaḷil kāṇappaṭum vaṭa coṟkaḷuḷ cirakam-pāttiram (51), kāraṇam (60), tampalam (65), picācar (65), kuṇaṅkaḷ (71), vayantakam-oruvaki aṇi (79), āram-māli (71), nūpuram-cilampu (83), vacciram (105), nēmi (105), cāmaṉ, kāmaṉ (94), mēkali (96), utti (97), vicci-vitti (148) eṉpavi kuṟikkattakkavi.
kālam cellac cellat tamiḻar vāḻvil vaṭacoṟkaḷum vaṭanūṟ ceytikaḷum piṟavum eṅṅaṉam paṭippaṭiyāka mikuntuvantuḷḷaṉa eṉpati ikkalit tokic ceytikaḷ naṉku pulappaṭuttutal kāṇka.
15. paripāṭal
muṉṉuri
paripāṭal eṉṉum icippākkaḷāl tokukkap peṟṟamiyāl ippākkaḷiṉ tokuti paripāṭal eṉap peyar peṟṟatu. ippākkaḷukkup paṇkaḷ vakukkap peṟṟuḷḷatik kāṇa, ivi caṅka kālattil iciyuṭaṉ pāṭappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatu teḷivu.
itu, tokinili vakiyāl pā eṉṟu collappaṭum ilakkaṇam iṉṟi, ellāppāviṟkum potuvāka niṟṟaṟku uriyatāy iṉpap poruḷip paṟṟik kūṟum. itu ciṟumi irupattintu aṭiyum, perumi nāṉūṟu aṭiyum elliyākak koṇṭu varum, eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kūṟiyuḷḷār.1
paripāṭaṟkaṇ maliyum yāṟum ūrum varuṇikkappaṭum eṉṟu iḷampūraṇar kūṟiyuḷḷār.2 aṟam, poruḷ, iṉpam, vīṭu eṉṉum uṟutip poruḷkaḷ nāṉkaṉuḷ iṉpattiyē poruḷākak koṇṭu kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu, mali viḷiyāṭṭu, puṉal viḷiyāṭṭu mutaliyavaṟṟil ippāṭal varumeṉṟu pērāciriyar kūṟiyuḷḷār.3 teyva vāḻttu uṭpaṭak kāmapporuḷ kuṟittu ulakiyalē paṟṟi varum eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟiyuḷḷār.4
paripāṭal
iṉṟuḷḷa paripāṭalkaḷiṟ pala tirumāliyum murukaṉiyum paṟṟiyavi; pakti neṟi paṟṟiyavi; tolkāppiyar vitikku māṟupaṭṭavi. viyi paṟṟiya pāṭalkaḷum murukaṉ paṟṟiya pāṭalkaḷuḷ cilavumē akapporuḷ paṟṟiyavi. eṉavē, tolkāppiyarkkup piṟpaṭṭa kālattil puṟampaṟṟiya ceytikaḷum paripāṭalil pāṭappeṟṟaṉa eṉpatu ippākkaḷāl terikiṉṟatu.
iṉṟuḷḷa paripāṭal eṉṉum nūlil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷa pākkaḷ akam, puṟam ākiya iraṇṭiyum paṟṟiyavi. eṭṭuttoki nūlkaḷuḷ puṟanāṉūṟum patiṟṟup pattum puṟapporuḷ paṟṟiya nūlkaḷ eṉpatum, ēṉiya aintum akapporuḷ paṟṟiya nūlkaḷ eṉpatum muṉpē kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. akapporuḷ nūlkaḷ aintu tiṇikaḷip paṟṟip pēcum; āṉāl paripāṭalkaḷ aintu tiṇikaḷiyum nilikkaḷaṉākak koṇṭu akapporuḷ ceytikaḷik kūṟavilli. piṟa akapporuḷ nūlkaḷil pēraracar, ciṟṟaracar mutaliyōr paṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷ irukkum. āṉāl paripāṭalil avaṟṟip paṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷē illi. pāṇṭiyarkaḷip paṟṟiyum avarkaḷatu nāṭṭip paṟṟiyumē kuṟippukkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. paripāṭaliluḷḷa puṟapporuṭ pakutiyilum piṟa aracarkaḷip paṟṟip pēccē illi. tirumāliruñcōliyil uḷḷa tirumāl, tirupparaṅkuṉṟattu murukaṉ ākiya teyvaṅkaḷ paṟṟiya tōttirap pākkaḷākavē pala amintuḷḷaṉa. viyi paṟṟiya pākkaḷum, ataṟkut teyvattaṉmi kūṟi, ataṉi vaḻipaṭum pāṭalkaḷākavē amintuḷḷaṉa.
piṟa toki nūlkaḷil aintiṇik kaṭavuḷar āṅkāṅkuk kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉar; āyiṉ, paripāṭalil kuṟiñci nilak kaṭavuḷākiya murukaṉum, mulli nilak kaṭavuḷāṉa tirumālumē virivākap pāṭappeṟṟuḷḷaṉar. ittakiya pākkaḷi vēṟu toki nūlkaḷil kāṇal iyalātu. innūṟ pākkaḷiṉ naṭi eḷimi vāyntatu. ippākkaḷ varuṇaṉi mikuntavi. ovvoru pāviṟkum ici kūṟappaṭṭiruppatāl ivi iciyōṭu pāṭuvataṟku amintavi eṉpatu terikiṟatu. ātalāl ivi makkaḷuṭiya pēccu vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷiyum āṅkāṅkē peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. iṅṅaṉam ippāṭalkaḷ pā vakiyilum ceyyuḷ naṭiyilum varuṇaṉiyilum eṇṇattilum muṟivippilum piṟavaṟṟilum māṟupaṭṭuḷḷami aṟiyattakum.
aḻintaṉa pōka iṉṟu innūlil iruppavi 22 pāṭalkaḷākum; (tolkāppiya vuriyilum puṟattiraṭṭilum vēṟu mūṉṟu pāṭalkaḷ kiṭittuḷa.) ivaṟṟiyavi eṭṭu; viyi paṟṟiyavi eṭṭu; maturi, viyiyāṟu, tirupparaṅkuṉṟam, tirumāliruñ cōli mali ākiyavi paṟṟiya ceytikaḷē ippāṭalkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟi nōkka, innūṟpākkaḷ pāṇṭiya nāṭṭic ciṟappikkavē pāṭappeṟṟaṉa eṉṟu karuta iṭantarukiṉṟaṉa.
āciriyar nallantuvaṉār, iḷamperu vaḻutiyār, kaṭuvaṉ iḷeyiṉaṉār, karumpiḷḷip pūtaṉār, kīrantiyār, kuṉṟampūtaṉār, kēcavaṉār, nappaṇṇaṉār, nallac cutaṉār, nallaḻuciyār, nalleḻuṉiyār, nalvaḻutiyār, miyōṭak kōvaṉār eṉṉum patiṉmūṉṟu pēr innūṟ pāṭalkaḷip pāṭiyuḷḷaṉar. ovvoru pāṭalukkum ici vakutta pulavar vēṟāvar.
innūṟ pāṭalkaḷukkup parimalēḻakar iyaṟṟiya uri kiṭittuḷḷatu. ivvuri pala iṭaṅkaḷil poḻippuriyāyum, cila iṭaṅkaḷil patavuriyāyum, vēṟu cila iṭaṅkaḷil, karutturiyāyum amintuḷḷatu; ilakkaṇak kuṟippukkaḷi āṅkāṅkup peṟṟuḷḷatu. viḷaṅkāta pakutikaḷ cila tamiḻnūl mēṟkōḷkaḷālum vaṭanūl karuttukkaḷālum viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
ṭākṭar u. vē. cāminātiyar avarkaḷ innūlic ciṟanta muṟiyiṟ patippittaṉar. civacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakattār putiya uriyuṭaṉ kūṭiya patippu oṉṟi veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷaṉar.
paripāṭaliṉ kālam
paripāṭal caṅka kālattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭatu eṉṟu kūṟum aṟiñar viyāpurip piḷḷiyavarkaḷ ataṟkuriya taṭikaḷik kīḻvarumāṟu moḻikiṉṟaṉar:
taṭikaḷ  1. paripāṭalkaḷip pāṭiya pulavaruḷ oruvarēṉum piṟa toki nūlkaḷip pāṭiyavarāyt terintilar. cila paripāṭalkaḷip pāṭiya āciriyaṉ nallantuvaṉār, antuvaṉār-maturi āciriyaṉ, nallantuva ṉār eṉṉum pulavarkaḷiṉ vēṟāṉavar. iṅṅaṉamē patiṉintām paripāṭalip pāṭiya iḷam peruvaḻutiyār puṟanāṉūṟṟil 182 ām ceyyuḷip pāṭiya kaṭaluḷ māynta iḷamperuvaḻutiyiṉ vēṟāṉavar. ēṉeṉil, piṉṉavar tamatu puṟappāṭṭil intirar eṉṟu paṉmiyil cuṭṭalāl camaṇar eṉak karutaṟpālar ātaliṉ eṉka.5
2. piṟa toki nūlkaḷil kāṇappaṭum vaṭacoṟ kaḷiyum, purāṇa itikācak katikaḷiyumviṭap paripāṭalkaḷil ivviruvakiyum mikutiyāka iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. kaviti, ārātaṉi (pāṭal 6), mēkali, vākuvalayam (pāṭal 7), puṅkavam, nātar, kuṭāri, aruccippōr, amirtapāṉam (pāṭal 8), mituṉam, puṉṉākam, caṇpakam, kuntam, mallikāmāli (pāṭal 11), yāttiri, piramam, irati, cōpaṉam (pāṭal 19), vantikka, cintikka (pāṭal 20) mutaliya vaṭacoṟkaḷ paripāṭalkaḷiṉ piṟkālatti uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa.6
3. caṅkakālattiṟkup piṉpē tiruvēṅkaṭam, tiruvaraṅkam, tirumāliruñ cōlimali, tiruvaṉantapuram eṉpaṉa viṇavat taḷikaḷākak kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. piṉvanta āḻvārkaḷ ittaḷikaḷip pāṭiyuḷḷaṉar. cilappatikārat tilum ivi kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tirumāliruñcōli mali paripāṭalil kuṟikkappaṭalāl paripāṭaliṉ kālam ki. pi. 600 eṉṉalām.7
4. ki. pi. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭil vāḻnta campantar tirupparaṅkuṉṟattuc civaperumāṉip pāṭiyuḷḷār; āyiṉ, murukaṉippaṟṟip pāṭavilli. eṉavē, campantarukkup piṉpē murukaṉ kōvil uṇṭāyiṟṟu eṉṟu kūṟalām. paripāṭalil ammurukaṉip paṟṟiya pāṭalkaḷ iruttalāl appāṭal kaḷiṉ kālam ēṟattāḻak ki. pi. 700 eṉṉalām.8
5. ki. pi. 300kku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻilakkiyattil akattiyarip paṟṟiya pēccē illi. āṉāl, paripāṭalil akattiyar potiyil muṉivar (pāṭal 11)  eṉṟu kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷār.9
6. ki. pi. mūṉṟu allatu nāṉkām nūṟṟāṇṭil, maturiyi āṇṭa apiṣēka pāṇṭiyaṉ kālattil maturi nāṉmāṭakkūṭaleṉṟu peyar peṟṟatu. ēṉavē, kūṭalip pukaḻum paripāṭal ki. pi. mūṉṟu allatu nāṉkām nūṟṟāṇṭic cērntatākalām.10
7. patiṉōrām paripāṭalil kāṇappaṭum vāṉili paṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷi ārāyntu tiru. cuvāmikkaṇṇup piḷḷiyavarkaḷ appāṭaliṉ kālam ki. pi. 634 eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.11
8. nāṉ (pāṭal 20 vari 82), āyum (pāṭal 6 vari 71) eṉṉum piṟkālac colluruvaṅkaḷ paripāṭalkaḷil payiṉṟuḷḷaṉa.12
iṉi, ivaṟṟi ovvoṉṟāka iṅku ārāyvōm:
viṭikaḷ
1. paripāṭalkaḷukku icivakutta pulavarkaḷuḷ kaṇṇaṉār oruvar. puṟanāṉūṟṟil 218ām ceyyuḷiyum, naṟṟiṇiyil 79 ām ceyyuḷiyum kaṇṇakaṉār eṉṟa pulavar pāṭiyuḷḷār. ivviruvarum oruvarē eṉṟu ṭākṭar u. vē. cāminātiyar avarkaḷ karutukiṟārkaḷ.13 ikkarutti maṟuppataṟku evvitac cāṉṟum illi.
naṉṉakaṉāreṉṟa pulavar, puṟanāṉūṟṟil 381 ām ceyyuḷip pāṭiyuḷḷār. kīrantiyāratu paripāṭalukku icivakutta naṉṉākaṉār eṉṟa pulavarum muṉ kūṟappeṟṟa naṉṉākaṉārum oruvarē eṉṟu karutuvatil tavaṟilli. icip pulavarkaḷ iyaṟṟamiḻp pulavarāyum iruttal kēcavaṉārik koṇṭu aṟiyalām.
iṅṅaṉamē iyaṟṟamiḻp pulavar icittamiḻilum vallarātalic cilappatikāram koṇṭu aṟiyalām. eṉavē mēlē kāṭṭappeṟṟa icippulavar iruvarum caṅkakālap pulavar allar eṉṟu uṟutiyākak kūṟa iyalāmi kāṇka.
2. piṟatoki nūlkaḷilum uruttiraṉ, ulōccaṉār, pavuttiraṉ, piramatattaṉ, kācipavaṉ, kīraṉār, cattinātaṉār, piramacāri, kucikaṉār, tāmōtaraṉār, mārkkaṇṭēyaṉār, vāṉmīkiyār pōṉṟa vaṭamoḻip peyarkaḷ pulavar peyarkaḷāka vantuḷḷaṉa. piṟa toki nūlkaḷil murukaṉ cūrapatumaṉik koṉṟami, civaṉ muppuram erittami, tirumāl piramaṉip paṭittami pōṉṟa purāṇa itikācak katikaḷ āṅkāṅku iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. kaṇṇaṉ nappiṉṉiyi maṇantami, kaṇṇaṉ kuruntu ōcittatu pōṉṟa pākavata nikaḻccikaḷ cilappatikāram āycciyar kuraviyuḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷa. kaṇṇaṉum palatēvaṉum nappiṉṉiyi naṭuviṟkoṇṭu āṭiya aṟputak kuravi āyarpāṭiyil āṭappaṭṭatu eṉṟum āycciyar kuravi aṟivikkiṟatu. kaṇṇaṉ nīrāṭikkoṇṭirunta āyarmakaḷir āṭikaḷik kavarntamiyum appoḻutu palarāmaṉ varutaliyaṟintu, āyar makaḷiratu māṉaṅ kākka vēṇṭik kaṇṇaṉ kuruntu ōcittut taṉ ceyalit tamiyaṉ aṟiyāmaṟ ceytamiyum, akanāṉūṟṟil (ce. 59) marutaṉ iḷanākaṉār,
. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .vaṭā atu
vaṇpuṉal toḻuni vārmaṇal akaṉṟuṟi
aṇṭar makaḷir taṇṭaḻi uṭīiyar
marañcela mititta māal
eṉṟu kuṟittuḷḷār.
iṉik kaṇṇaṉ āṭiya kuṭakkūttum, avaṉ makaṉāṉa pirattiyumnaṉ āṭiya pēṭu eṉṉum kūttum, kaṇṇaṉ mallaṉ vēṭam pūṇṭu vāṇaṉik koṉṟāṭiya mallāṭal kūttum, alliyat tokuti eṉṟa kūttum, cilappatikāra-araṅkēṟṟu kātiyil iṭam peṟṟuḷa. cilappatikāra kālattiṟku muṉṉarē ikkati tamiḻakattil vaḻakkup peṟṟuviṭṭaṉa eṉpatiyē uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa. ivipōṉṟa, cilavē ivi paripāṭalil tirumāl paṟṟiyum civaṉ paṟṟiyum pāṭappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ēṉavē, ivi koṇṭu nūliṉ kālam kāṇal arumiyilum arumi!
paripāṭalkaḷuḷ pala, tirumāl murukaṉ ākiya kaṭavuḷarip paṟṟiyavi. ātalāl, akkaṭavuḷarippaṟṟi vaṭamoḻip purāṇaṅkaḷil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa ceytikaḷit taṉṉakattē koṇṭuḷḷaṉa. cuṅka marapiṉar kālattil ki. mu. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil-vaṭa intiyāvil tōṉṟiya paktineṟi tamiḻakattilum naṉku paraviviṭṭa kāraṇattālum, kaṭavuḷarpaṟṟiya ceytikaḷ vaṭamoḻi nūlkaḷilēyē mikutiyāka iṭam peṟṟirunta kāraṇattālum, kaṭavuḷar paṟṟiya paripāṭalkaḷil vaṭamoḻic coṟkaḷum purāṇak katikaḷum ōraḷavu mikutiyāka iṭam peṟṟiruttal iyalputāṉē! iraṭṭik kāppiyaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟāṉa maṇimēkali putta camayattip paṟṟiyē pēca eḻuntatu. ātalāl, ataṉkaṇ vaṭamoḻic coṟkaḷ mikutiyākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avvoru kāraṇam paṟṟiyē maṇimēkali cilappatikāra kālattiṟkum muṟpaṭṭatu eṉṟu kūṟuvatu poruntātu aṉṟō?
3. cilappatikārattiṉ kālam kayavākuviṉ kālamākiya 
ki. pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakuti (ki. pi. 113-136) eṉpatu muṉpu pala iṭaṅkaḷil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. cilappatikārattil vēṅkaṭam, tiruvaraṅkam, aḻakarmali ākiya mūṉṟum tirumālukku uriyaṉavākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pattuppāṭuṭaḷ oṉṟāṉa perum pāṇāṟṟuppaṭi, akkālak kāñcikku arukil paḷḷi koṇṭa tirumāl kōvil iruntati iyampukiṟatu.
	kāntaḷañ cilampiṟ kaḷiṟupaṭin tāṅkup
	pāmpaṇip paḷḷi yamarntōṉ āṅkaṇ 	(vari 372-373)
4. tirupparaṅkuṉṟattil civaṉip pāṭiya campantar aṅkuḷḷa murukaṉip pāṭavilli eṉpatu iṉṟuḷḷa tiruppatikattik koṇṭu terikiṟatu. avar paraṅkuṉṟattip paṟṟip pāṭiya orē patikamtāṉ kiṭittuḷḷatu. campantar pāṭavilli eṉpatu koṇṭu avar kālattil tirupparaṅkuṉṟattil murukaṉ kōvil illiyeṉṟu kūṟuvatu poruntātu. ēṉeṉil. akanāṉūṟṟil
cūrmaruṅku aṟutta cuṭarili neṭuvēl
ciṉamiku murukaṉ taṇparaṅ kuṉṟattu
antuvaṉ pāṭiya cantukeḻu neṭuvari
eṉṟu maturi marutaṉ iḷanākaṉār pāṭiyuḷḷār. immarutaṉ iḷanākaṉārkku muṉpē antuvaṉ eṉṟa pulavar tirupparaṅ kuṉṟattu murukaṉip pāṭiyuḷḷār eṉpatu ivvaṭikaḷāl teḷivākiṟataṉṟō!
	neṭunal yāṉi aṭupōrc ceḻiyaṉ
	koṭinuṭaṅku maṟukiṟ kūṭaṟ kuṭāatu
	palpoṟi maññi velkoṭi yuyariya
	oṭiyā viḻaviṉ neṭiyōṉ kuṉṟam
eṉṉum akanāṉūṟṟu 149 ām pāṭal aṭikaḷum, caṅka kālattilēyē tirupparaṅkuṉṟattil murukaṉ kōvil iruntati meyppikkiṉṟaṉa; aṉṟiyum akkōvilil akkālattil ōyviṉṟi viḻākkaḷ naṭipeṟṟamiyiyum ivvarikaḷ pulappaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē, tirupparaṅkuṉṟattil murukaṉ kōvil caṅka kālattilēyē iruntatu eṉpatu teḷivātal kāṇka.
5. 	teṉṉavaṟ peyariya tuṉṉarun tuppiṟ
	ṟoṉmutu kaṭavuḷ
eṉavarum maturikkāñci aṭikaṭku (vari. 40-41)-
irāvaṇaṉit tamiḻnāṭṭi yāḷātapaṭi pōkkiṉa kiṭṭutaṟkariya valiyiṉiyuṭiya paḻimi mutirnta akattiyaṉ eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uri kūṟiyuḷḷār. in nacciṉārkkiṉiyarē tolkāppiyap pāyira vuriyil akattiyar potiyiṉkaṇ iruntu, irāvaṇaṉik kantaruvattāṟ piṇittu, irākkatari āṇṭu iyaṅkāmi vilakki, eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.
mēlē kūṟappeṟṟa maturikkāñci aṭikaḷukku nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uriyit tanta pattuppāṭṭiṉ patip pāciriyarākiya ṭākṭar u. vē. cāminātiyar avarkaḷ aṭikkuṟippil, teṉṉāṭṭi āṇṭu kuṭikaḷit tuṉpuṟuttivanta irāvaṇaṉi, akattiyar potiyamali urukumpaṭi icipāṭi ilaṅkikkup pōkkiṉar eṉpatu paṇṭi varalāṟu eṉṟu kūṟi, akattiyar iciyil vallunar eṉpataṟkum, akattiyar yāḻ vācittup potiyiṉi urukac ceytaṉar eṉpataṟkum, irāvaṇaṉi icipāṭi aṭakkiṉar eṉpataṟkum, tirukkayilāya ñāṉavuri, takkayākap paraṇi, cōṇacayili māli, veṅkiyulā, tañcivāṇaṉ kōvi eṉṉum ilakkiyaṅkaḷiliruntu mēṟkōḷ kāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar. ivi maturikkāñcikku mikap piṟpaṭṭa nūlkaḷāyiṉum, akattiyar icipāṭi irāvaṇaṉi viraṭṭiṉār eṉṟa varalāṟu innāṭṭil neṭuka vaḻaṅkivantatu eṉṉum uṇmiyi uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa allavā? eṉavē akattiyar toki nūlkaḷiṟ kuṟikkappeṟavilli eṉpatu poruntāmi kāṇka.
patiṉōrām paripāṭalil potiyiṉ muṉivaṉ akattiyar kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷār. ki. pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil pāṭappeṟṟa cilappatikāram eṭṭām kātiyil (vari 8) `potiyiṉ māmuṉi eṉṉum toṭar akattiyaric cuṭṭutalik kāṇalām.
6. māṭam piṟaṅkiya malipukaḻk kūṭal eṉavarum maturik kāñci aṭiyum (vari 429), periya nāṉmāṭattālē malinta pukaḻik kūṭutaliyuṭiya maturi eṉavarum nacciṉārk kiṉiyar uriyum kāṇattakkavi. ivi, caṅkakālattilēyē kūṭalil (nāṉ) māṭam iruntati meyppikkiṉṟaṉa.
kalittoki 92 ām pāṭalil nāṉmāṭak kūṭal eṉpatu vantuḷḷatu. kalittokiyiṉ kālam ki. pi. 300 eṉak koṇṭālum, atu caṅka nūlātal uṟutiyē. eṉavē, nāṉmāṭak kūṭal eṉpatu koṇṭu paripāṭal caṅkakālattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭatu eṉak kūṟal iyalātu.
7. aṟiñar cuvāmikkaṇṇup piḷḷiyavarkaḷ paripāṭalil varum vāṉilipaṟṟiya ceyti koṇṭu kūṟiya kālam poruntātu eṉpatit takka kāraṇam kāṭṭip pērāciriyar pi. ṭi. cīnivāca ayyaṅkār maṟuttuḷḷār eṉpatu iṅku aṟiyattakum.15
8. nāṉ, āmām mutaliya coṟkaḷ potu makkaḷāl pēcappaṭṭa vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷ eṉṉalām. avi camayattil peṟṟuḷḷa celvākkiṉāl cila camayaṅkaḷil pulavar pākkaḷil iṭam peṟutal iyaṟki. nām vāḻum ikkālattil pulavar cilar ilakkiyac coṟkaḷiyē payaṉpaṭutti nūl eḻutuvatum, pulavar palar eḷimikarutip pēccu vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷi mikuyākap payaṉpaṭuttalum iyalpāka irukkiṉṟatu. paripāṭalkaḷuḷ pala vaḻipāṭṭup pāṭalkaḷ potu makkaḷukku uriyavi. eṉavē, avaṟṟukku iciyum vakukkappaṭṭatu. ātalāl makkaḷ pēccu vaḻakkilirunta nāṉ, āmām eṉṟa coṟkaḷ iṭam peṟṟaṉa eṉṟu koḷvatu poruttamākum.
kaṇṇaṉ tamiyaṉāṉa palatēvaṉukkup paṉik koṭiyum nāñciṟpaṭiyum uriyavi. avi iraṇṭum tirumālukku uriyaviyākap paripāṭalkaḷ (4, 13) kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. mēlum palatēvaṉi vācutēvaṉuṭaṉ (kaṇṇaṉuṭaṉ) iṇittu vaḻipaṭṭami paripāṭalil (13) kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. iṅṅaṉam iruvariyum iṇittu vaḻipaṭum muṟi mikat toṉmiyāṉatu.
vaṭintiyāvil kōcuṇṭi, nāṉākāṭ eṉṉum iṭaṅkaḷiṟ kiṭitta kalveṭṭukkaḷil vācutēvaṉ-palatēvaṉ vaṇakkam iṇintē kāṇappaṭu kiṟatu. ikkal veṭṭukkaḷ muṟiyē ki. mu. 3, 1 ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷic cērntavi. eṉavē, vācutēvaṉ-pala tēvaṉ vaṇakkam vaṭa intiyāvil ki. mu. 3 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil iruntami naṉku teḷivākum.16
ivvaṇakkam caṅkakālat tamiḻakattilum iruntatu; kaṇṇaṉukkum palatēvaṉukkum kōvilkaḷ iruntaṉa. pulavarkaḷ, nī pakivarik kolvatil kaṇṇaṉiyum valimiyil palatēvaṉiyum oppi17 eṉat tamiḻaracari vāḻttiṉar. iruperun teyvamum oruṅkiruntāṅku,18 eṉṟu avarkaḷi iraṇṭu perun teyvaṅkaḷākavē karuti vaḻippaṭṭaṉar eṉpaṉa puṟanāṉūṟu, cilappatikāram mutaliya caṅka nūlkaḷāl aṟiyappaṭum. palatēvaṉukkup paṉikkoṭi uriyatu. tolkāppiyar paṉi eṉṉum peyar muṉ koṭi eṉpatu varuvataṟkut taṉi viti kūṟiyuḷḷati nōkki, avar kālattilēyē ivvaṇakkam tamiḻakattil iruntatu eṉṟu niṉittal takum.
ki. mu. mutal nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē caṅkaruṭaṇa vācu tēvarkaḷ (palatēvarum, vācutēvarum) marāttiyarāl vaḻipaṭap paṭṭaṉar. itē kālattil immatam teṟkilum paraviyiruttal vēṇṭum. paripāṭal mutaliya nūlkaḷ akkālattil eḻuntaṉa ākalām.19
caṅkakālam eṉpatu muṉpu kūṟiyāṅku mikap parantupaṭṭa kāla elliyi uṭiyatu. ap parantupaṭṭa kāla elliyil ki. mu. nāṉkām nūṟṟāṇṭil tolkāppiyam ceyyap paṭṭatu-ataṟku muṉpu cilavum piṉpu palavumākat tokinūṟ pāṭalkaḷ pala kālaṅkaḷiṟ pāṭap peṟṟaṉa eṉpatum, piṟpaṭṭa colluruvaṅkaḷiyum, vaḻakkukaḷiyum purāṇa katikaḷiyum mikutiyākak koṇṭa kalittoki, paripāṭal, cilappatikāram, maṇimēkali eṉpaṉa ki. mu. iraṇṭām, mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭu kaḷiṟ ceyyap peṟṟirukkalām eṉpatum poruttamākum.
paripāṭalkaḷip pāṭiya pulavar iṉṟuḷḷa piṟa toki nūṟpākkaḷip pāṭiya pulavarē eṉak koḷḷiṉum, appulavariṉ vēṟāṉavar eṉak koḷḷiṉum, avarkaḷ kālam ki. pi. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟuti eṉṟu koḷvatu takkatākum.
kalittokiyil-naṟṟiṇi pōṉṟa nūlkaḷil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa mūvēntar pōrcceyalkaḷum, ciṟṟaracar pōrc ceyalkaḷum, ivviru tarattārip paṟṟiya piṟa vivaraṅkaḷum iṭam peṟavilli. pāṇṭiyar, avar tam talinakarākiya kūṭal, viyiyāṟu eṉpaviyē iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ātalāl kalittokip pākkaḷ nakkīrar, kapilar, paraṇar pōṉṟa pulavar perumakkaḷukkup piṉpē pāṭappeṟṟaṉavātal vēṇṭum eṉpatu muṉpē kūṟappaṭṭa taṉṟō! paripāṭalilum itē nilimi kāṇappaṭuvatāl, itaṉ kālamum kalit tokiyiṉ kālameṉṟē kūṟutal poruttamākum.
paripāṭal ceytikaḷ
mupporuḷ paṟṟiya pāṭalkaḷ
paripāṭal nūlil irupattiraṇṭu pāṭalkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ āṟu (1-4, 13, 15) tirumālip paṟṟiyavi; eṭṭup pāṭalkaḷ (5, 8, 9, 14, 17, 18, 19, 21) cevvēḷip paṟṟiyavi; eṭṭup pāṭalkaḷ (6, 7, 10, 11, 12, 16, 20, 22) viyi āṟṟip paṟṟiyavi. ivi allāmal tolkāppiya uriyil kāṇap paṭṭa mūṉṟu pāṭalkaḷ innūliṉ iṟutiyil cērkkappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu tirumāl paṟṟiyatu; oṉṟu viyi paṟṟiyatu; maṟṟoṉṟu maturi paṟṟiyatu.
tirumālip paṟṟiya 6 pākkaḷilum akapporuḷ paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ illi; cevvēḷip paṟṟiya 8 ceyyuṭkaḷil nāṉku (8, 9, 14, 21) akapporuḷ kalantavi; viyi paṟṟiya eṭṭup pākkaḷil āṟu pāṭalkaḷ akapporuḷ kalantavi. ivvakapporuḷ kalanta pāṭalkaḷ talivaṉatu parattimi yiyum, talivi ataṉik kaṭitaliyum, talivaṉ poyccūḷ puritaliyum, tōḻi ataṟkāka avaṉik kaṭitaliyum, talivaṉ taliviyōṭu viyiyiṉ putuveḷḷattil nīrāṭiyatiyum, talivaṉ iṟparattiyuṭaṉ nīrāṭiyatiyum, talivi-paratti uriyāṭaliyum paṟṟik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa.
iruṅkuṉṟam: iḵtu iṉṟiya aḻakarmaliyāka iruttal kūṭum eṉpatu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. iruṅkuṉṟattiṉ mītu tirumālukkum palatēvaṉukkum oru kōvil iruntatu. ataṉāl ikkuṉṟam māliruṅkuṉṟam eṉappaṭṭatu; akkuṉṟattil cōlikaḷ mikkiruntamiyil tirumāliruñ cōlimali eṉappaṭṭatu (pāṭal 15, vari 13-23). ammaliyil cilampāṟu eṉṟa peyaruṭaṉ oru periya aruvi viḷaṅkiyatu. malimītuḷḷa kōvilil kuḻalōciyum, miṭaṟṟup pāṭalum, tāḷa oliyum, muḻavoliyum nilaviyiruntaṉa. makkaḷ kuṭumpam kuṭumpamākac ceṉṟu maliyēṟit tirumāliyum palatēvaṉiyum vaṇaṅkiṉar (15, vari 30-45). kaṇṇaṉum palatēvaṉum perumpeyar iruvar eṉappaṭṭaṉar (15, vari 66).
iruntiyūr: iruntiyūr eṉpatu kūṭalukku appāl viyik kariyil irunta ūrākum. maturiyil iṉṟu kūṭalaḻakar kōvil uḷḷa iṭamē akkāla iruntiyūr eṉpatu. iṅkut tirumāl irunta kōlattil, kāṭci tarukiṟār. ataṉāl ittalam irunta ūr eṉṟu peyar peṟṟatu. appeyar kālappōkkil iruntiyūr eṉa māṟi vaḻaṅkalāyiṟṟu.
taṇparaṅ kuṉṟam: itu kūṭal nakarattiṟku mēṟkil iruntatu. malimītu cōlikaḷ mikkiruntaṉa. maliyiliruntu aruvikaḷ kīḻnōkkip pāyntaṉa. ït‰whš m«kiy j©gu§F‹w« vd¥g£lJ.(14, 13-17). malimītu murukaṉ tirukkōvil iruntatu. pāṇar yāḻ vācittaṉar. palavaki icikkaruvikaḷ olittaṉa. viṟaliyar naṭaṉamāṭiṉar. makkaḷ iṟivaṉi vaḻipaṭṭaṉar (17, 9-21).
viyi: viyiyil putuveḷḷam varuvatik kaṇṭatum āṟṟaṅkariyiṉ kāvalar kariyik kāval kāttaṉar (10, 7) maturi makkaḷ aṉivarum putu veḷḷattil nīrāṭiṉar. talivar cilar parattiyaruṭaṉ nīrāṭiṉar. nīrāṭiyavarkaḷ poṉṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa natti; naṇṭu, iṟāl, vāḷi mīṉ ivaṟṟi nīril viṭṭaṉar (10, 71-88). viyiyiṉ kariyilirunta cōlikaḷil āṭal pāṭal nikaḻntaṉa (22, 35-45). maturi makkaḷ viyiyip palavāṟu vāḻttit tattam illaṅkaḷi aṭintaṉar.
tamiḻ viyi: pāṇṭiyar tamiḻc caṅkatti niṟuviyavar. pulavarkaḷi ātarittut tamiḻi vaḷarttavar ataṉāl avarkaḷ talinakarākiya kūṭal tamiḻ keḻukūṭal eṉap paṭṭatu. ak kūṭali aṭuttup pāynta viyiyum. tamiḻ viyi (6, 60) eṉappaṭṭatu.
tin nīrāṭal: mārkaḻi mātattil-niṟimati nāḷākiya tiru ātiriyil ākamaṅkaḷi uṇarnta pūcakarkaḷ teyvattiṟkut tiruviḻāvit toṭaṅkiṉar. appōtu kaṉṉippeṇkaḷ oṉṟukūṭi, nilam maḻi vaḷam peṟṟuk kuḷirvatākuka; eṉṟu colli, ampā āṭali mēṟ koṇṭaṉar. annōṉpu muṟiyi aṟinta mutiya pārppaṉimār akkaṉṉip peṇkaḷukku nōṉpu nōṟkum muṟimiyit terivittaṉar. ak kaṉṉippeṇkaḷ, avarkaḷ terivittapaṭi paṉi niṟinta katiravaṉ tōṉṟum kālattil viyiyil nīrāṭiṉar; kariyil taṅkiyirunta antaṇar vaḷartta vēḷvittīyi vaṇaṅkiṉar. iṅṅaṉam iḷiya āṇmakkaḷōṭu kāmak kuṟippu illāta viḷiyāṭṭip puriyum ak kaṉṉip peṇkaḷ tavamākiya tin nīrāṭali vāykkap peṟṟaṉar (11, vari 74-92). itu ammaṉ poruṭṭu nikaḻttap peṟum nīrāṭṭu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum. ampā, ampāḷ eṉpavi ammaṉik kuṟippavi.20
aṭiyār vēṇṭutal: aṭiyavar, nāṅkaḷ eṅkaḷ cuṟṟattāruṭaṉ niṉ tiruvaṭikaḷit toḻutu vāḻa aruḷ purivāyāka (1), yām meyyuṇarvu peṟṟu niṉṉi vaṇaṅka aruḷ purivāy (2), inta iruṅkuṉṟattiṉ aṭiyiṉkaṇ vāḻum pēṟu eṅkaḷukkuk kiṭippatākuka, (15) eṉṟu tirumāli vēṇṭiṉar.
aṭiyār cilar, yām vēṇṭupavi poruḷum poṉṉum pōkamum alla; emakku vīṭu payakkum niṉ aruḷum, ataṉi uṇṭākka niṉṉiṭattu yām ceyyum aṉpum, avviraṇṭālum varum aṟamum ākiya im mūṉṟumēyākum (5). niṉ tirupparaṅkuṉṟattil yām vāḻutal nāḷtōṟum polintu payaṉ taruvatōṭu ciṟakkumpaṭi niṉ aruḷi vēṇṭukiṟōm (9), piṟavit tuṉpam emmiviṭṭu nīṅkum eṉṟu niṉ pukaḻi ēttip pāṭuvōm (17), yām ematu cuṟṟattuṭaṉ kūṭi niṉ aṭikkaṇ vāḻutal iṉṟupōl eṉṟum poruntuka, (21) eṉṟu murukaṉi vēṇṭiṉar.
pirinta talivar viṉimuṭittu virivil vantu tammik kūṭavēṇṭum eṉpataṟkut taliviyar tirupparaṅkuṉṟattil viḻā iyaṟṟiṉar; piṉpu viyik kariyilum vaḻipāṭu ceytaṉar; piṉpu kūṭalil viruntu naṭattiṉar (17, vari 42-46).
ōviyam: ōviyam eḻutuvōṉ vallōṉ (21 vari 27-28) eṉappaṭṭāṉ. taṇparaṅkuṉṟattu mali mītirunta murukaṉ tirukkōvil maṇṭapattil pala ōviyaṅkaḷ variyap paṭṭiruntaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, nāḷ mīṉkaḷiyum tārakikaḷiyum uṭiya turuva cakkarattip poruntiya cūriyaṉ mutalāka uḷḷa kōḷkaḷiṉ nilimiyi viḷakkum ōviyam oṉṟākum. irati maṉmataṉik kuṟikkum ōviyam maṟṟoṉṟākum. koḷatamaṉ, akaliki, intiraṉ uruvaṅkaḷiyum, intiraṉ eṭutta pūṉi uruvattiyum vēṟōr ōviyam uṇarttiṉatu. ittakiya ōviyaṅkaḷ pala ammaṇṭapattil tīṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa (19, vari 46-53) ōviyaṅkaḷippaṟṟiya ittakiya teḷivāṉa kuṟippu vēṟu caṅka nūlkaḷil illi eṉpatu iṅku aṟiyattakum.
pēreṇkaḷ: neytal, kuvaḷi, āmpal, caṅkam, kamalam, veḷḷam eṉpavi nīṇṭa kālkaḷik kuṟikkum pēreṇkaḷ ākum (2, vari 13-14). tirumālpaṟṟiya taṉippāṭal oṉṟil tōḻam eṉpatu oru pērēṇ eṉṟu kuṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷatu. oruki, iruki, mukki. . . . . . . eṇki, oṉpatiṟṟukki, patiṟṟukki, āyiram ki, patiṉāṟāyiram ki, nūṟāyiram ki eṉa oṉṟu mutal nūṟāyiram vari eṇkaḷ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa (3, vari 34-43).
mēṟkōḷ: piṟa akapporuḷ nūlkaḷil uḷḷavāṟē paripāṭalilum tirukkuṟaḷ pōṉṟa nūlkaḷiṉ karuttukkaḷ iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ cilavaṟṟik kīḻē kāṇka.
